Conclusion: 该工作以四算子消失原理统一处理了Fong-Tsui型自伴性判定，摆脱传统附加假设，并提供显式的稳定性估计。作者亦指出大语言模型被用于辅助证明开发、代数计算、数值检验与论证审计。

Abstract: We present a general proof of the Fong-Tsui conjecture for bounded operators on arbitrary complex Hilbert spaces. Specifically, we show that $|T|\leq|\operatorname{Re}T|$ implies that $T$ is self-adjoint. The argument combines a positive inverse of a Sylvester map with a spectral cutoff determined by the norm of the positive defect $|\operatorname{Re}T|-|T|$. A local vanishing lemma reduces the analysis to the classical squared self-adjointness criterion, while positivity of the defect yields a global norm contradiction. We formulate the argument as an abstract four-operator vanishing principle, without compactness, trace, or separability assumptions. We also establish a quantitative stability estimate: if $|T|\leq|\operatorname{Re}T|+\varepsilon I$ and $0\leq\varepsilon\leq|T|$, then $|\operatorname{Im}T|\leq6|T|^{7/8}\varepsilon^{1/8}$. The constant is independent of the dimension, and the exponent is not claimed to be optimal. Large language models (LLMs) were used to assist with proof development, algebraic calculations, numerical checks, and auditing of the arguments.

</details>


### [93] [Zonoids whose polars are zonoids: the Banach--Mazur distance need not tend to one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10852)
*Dmitry Ryabogin,Artem Zvavitch*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究由Vitale与Mathis工作中的高斯旋转体（Gaussian zonoid of revolution）$Z_n$，证明其对偶$Z_n^*$也是zonoid，并计算其到欧氏球的Banach–Mazur距离约为$1.10$且与维数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究zonoid的对偶是否仍为zonoid，以及zonoid与其对偶均为zonoid时，相对于欧氏球的Banach–Mazur距离能否趋近于1。

Method: 以高斯旋转体$Z_n$（$n\geq2$）为对象，证明$Z_n^*$为zonoid，并计算$Z_n$到欧氏球的Banach–Mazur距离。

Result: 证明$Z_n^*$仍为zonoid；其到欧氏球的Banach–Mazur距离与维数无关，约为$1.10$。

Conclusion: 对偶为zonoid的zonoid族中，其上的Banach–Mazur距离上确界并不趋于1。该构造还给出一个可分的实Banach空间$X$，$X$非欧氏空间等距，但$X$与$X^*$均可线性等距嵌入$L_1$。

Abstract: For every $n\geq2$, we consider a Gaussian zonoid of revolution $Z_n$ arising from works of Vitale and Mathis. We prove that $Z_n^*$ is also a zonoid and compute the Banach--Mazur distance from $Z_n$ to the Euclidean ball. This distance is independent of the dimension and is approximately $1.10$. Consequently, the supremal Banach--Mazur distance among zonoids whose polars are zonoids does not converge to $1$. The construction also produces a separable real Banach space $X$, not isometric to a Hilbert space, such that both $X$ and $X^*$ embed linearly isometrically into $L_1$.

</details>


### [94] [Typical dynamical properties of operators on $\ell_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10957)
*Jian Li,Qijing Liao*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究了$\ell_p$空间上范数不超过$M$的有界线性算子集合$\mathcal{L}_M(X)$中典型算子的动力学性质，并刻画了双边加权后移具体族的典型性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨超循环算子在算子空间中"典型"（Baire范畴意义下剩余）行为，明确$\mathcal{L}_M(X)$上典型算子具有哪些动力学性质。

Method: 在$\ell_p$（$1<p<\infty$）上赋予SOT$^*$拓扑，使用Baire范畴与剩余集（典型性）方法研究$\mathcal{L}_M(X)$；同时对具体族$\mathcal{M}=\{I+B_w\}$赋予范数拓扑进行分析。

Result: 关于SOT$^*$，$\mathcal{L}_M(X)$中典型算子$T$是弱混合的、与给定超循环算子$S$弱不相交、非拓扑遍历，且对任意$k\geq2$，$(T,T^2,\dots,T^k)$是不相交超循环的。此外刻画了族$\mathcal{M}$在范数拓扑下的典型动力学性质。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{L}_M(X)$中算子典型地同时具备弱混合、与$S$弱不相交、非拓扑遍历以及多算子组不相交超循环等性质；具体族$\mathcal{M}$的典型性质亦被刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the typical dynamical properties of hypercyclic operators in $\mathcal{L}_M(X)$, the set of all bounded linear operators on $X$ whose norms are at most $M$, when $X=\ell_p$, $1< p<\infty$. We show that, with respect to SOT$^*$, a typical operator $T\in \mathcal{L}_M(X)$ is weakly mixing, is weakly disjoint from a given hypercyclic operator $S$, is not topologically ergodic, and satisfies $(T,T^2,\dotsc,T^k)$ is disjoint hypercyclic for any $k\geq 2$. We also study the typical dynamical properties for the concrete family $\mathcal{M}=\{I+B_w\in \mathcal{L}(X)\colon w\in c_0(\mathbb{Z})\}$, endowed with the norm topology, where $B_w$ is a bilateral weighted backward shift.

</details>


### [95] [A bi-Lipschitz characterization of strong minimum-attainment for Lipschitz maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11080)
*Geunsu Choi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了强最小可达Lipschitz函数稠密性，用双Lipschitz嵌入给出了充要条件，并给出纯1-不可求长性的等距刻画及向量值反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强范数可达Lipschitz函数已有研究，但其"最小"对偶版本——强最小可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性尚缺乏完整刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白并建立与度量几何性质的联系。

Method: 通过双Lipschitz嵌入与1维Hausdorff测度刻画稠密性：证明在完备度量空间M上强最小可达Lipschitz函数集不稠密，当且仅当M双Lipschitz等价于R中正Lebesgue测度子集（等价于M可双Lipschitz嵌入R且具有正1维Hausdorff测度）；并借助双Lipschitz副本上的强最小可达映射给出纯1-不可求长性的等距刻画。

Result: 得到稠密性失效的充要条件，即M双Lipschitz等价于R中正测度子集；由此获得M纯1-不可求长性的等距刻画；并构造若干反例说明主要结果无法自然推广到向量值情形。

Conclusion: 强最小可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性与空间的1维测度几何（双Lipschitz嵌入R、正Hausdorff测度）紧密相关，可实现纯1-不可求长性的等距刻画，但该结论在向量值框架下不成立。

Abstract: We completely characterize the denseness of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz functions, a minimum analogue for strongly norm-attaining Lipschitz functions, in terms of bi-Lipschitz embeddings. More precisely, our main result shows that the set of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz functions defined on a complete metric space $M$ fails the denseness if and only if $M$ is bi-Lipschitz equivalent to a subset of $\mathbb{R}$ with positive Lebesgue measure, or equivalently, if $M$ admits a bi-Lipschitz embedding into $\mathbb{R}$ and $M$ has positive 1-dimensional Hausdorff measure. As a consequence, we provide an isometric characterization of the pure 1-unrectifiability of $M$ in terms of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz maps defined on bi-Lipschitz copies of closed subsets of $M$. Several counterexamples showing that the main result cannot be naturally extended to the vector-valued setting are also presented.

</details>


### [96] [On weak dominance of t-conorms over t-norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11150)
*Shuangquan Li,Yao Ouyang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了连续t-conorm弱支配连续t-norm的条件，先归结为序数和算子的Archimedean分量问题，再用加性生成元统一表达该弱支配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合算子间（尤其t-norm与t-conorm间）的弱支配关系是聚合算子理论的重要课题。虽然Archimedean及连续情形已有若干刻画，但"连续t-conorm弱支配连续t-norm"的一般性判据尚未完全澄清。

Method: 将序数和（ordinal sum）算子的弱支配问题归约为其单个Archimedean分量的相应问题，再完全用这些分量的加性生成元刻画弱支配条件；方法统一覆盖严格（strict）与幂零（nilpotent）两种情形。

Result: 给出了"连续t-conorm弱支配连续t-norm"的完整刻画；该刻画以加性生成元形式表达，并可将已知的Archimedean算子结果作为特例包含。

Conclusion: 本文为连续t-conorm对连续t-norm的弱支配提供了完整、统一的一般判据，弥补了Archimedean情形之外的理论空白，并将已有Archimedean结果纳入统一框架。

Abstract: The weak dominance of aggregation operators, particularly between triangular norms (t-norms) and triangular conorms (t-conorms), has attracted considerable attention in aggregation operator theory. While several characterizations have been obtained for Archimedean and continuous cases, a general criterion for continuous t-conorms over continuous t-norms remains to be fully clarified. In this paper, we provide a complete characterization of a continuous t-conorm weakly dominating a continuous t-norm. We first reduce the problem for ordinal sum operators to that for their single Archimedean components, and then express the weak dominance condition entirely in terms of the additive generators of these components. Our approach covers both strict and nilpotent cases uniformly, and recovers the known results for Archimedean operators as a special case.

</details>


### [97] [Saturation and Localization Results for Max-product Generalized Sampling Operators based on Centered Bell-shaped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11157)
*Lorenzo Boccali,Gianluca Vinti*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究 max-product 广义采样算子对非负有界一致连续函数的逼近，建立了饱和阶、局部逆定理与强局部化结果，并将 Coroianu 和 Gal 关于特定 sinc 核的结论推广到更一般的核函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于 max-product 广义采样算子逼近性质的研究多局限于特定 sinc 型核函数，缺乏对一般核函数的系统性刻画。本文旨在在更广泛核函数假设下刻画其最优逼近速率（饱和现象）及局部逆性质。

Method: 在核函数为偶数中心钟形函数的假设下，分析 max-product 广义采样算子对 R 上非负有界一致连续函数的均匀逼近，确定饱和阶与饱和类；并研究在紧区间上以 1/w 速率逼近时函数局部 Lipschitz 连续性，以及针对 [0,1] 上严格正有界函数给出截断型算子的强局部化结果。

Result: 证明 1/w（w>0）为均匀饱和阶，且饱和类恰为非负常数函数，表明非平凡函数无法获得快于 1/w 的收敛速率；证明 Lipschitz 连续函数 1/w 逼近阶可局部逆转：若在 [a,b] 上以 1/w 逼近，则 f 在相应子区间上 Lipschitz 连续；并建立截断 max-product 广义采样算子序列的强局部化结果。

Conclusion: 结果将 Coroianu 和 Gal 仅针对特定 sinc 型核的结论推广到更广泛的核函数类，深化了对 max-product 广义采样算子饱和性与局部逼近行为的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the saturation order and a local inverse result for the uniform approximation of non-negative, bounded, and uniformly continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$ by max-product generalized sampling operators based on suitable kernel functions. In particular, assuming that the kernel is an even centered bell-shaped function, we first show that $1/w$, $w>0$, is the uniform saturation order, with the corresponding saturation class coinciding with the class of non-negative constant functions. This means that $1/w$ is the best possible rate of convergence that the max-product generalized sampling operators can achieve when approximating non-trivial (i.e., non-constant) non-negative, bounded, and uniformly continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$. Moreover, it is known that, for Lipschitz continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$, the approximation order is $1/w$ as $w \to +\infty$. Here, we show that this result can be locally reversed. Specifically, we prove that if $f$ can be approximated at the rate $1/w$ on a compact interval $[a,b]\subset\mathbb{R}$, then $f$ is Lipschitz continuous on $[a,c]$ for every $c \in [a,b)$ whenever $0<a<b$, and on $[c,b]$ for every $c \in (a,b]$ whenever $ a<b<0$. Finally, under the same assumptions on the kernel, we establish a strong localization result for sequences of truncated max-product generalized sampling operators in the case of strictly positive and bounded functions defined on $[0,1]$. All these results extend previous results of Coroianu and Gal, which were established only for specific sinc-type kernels, to a broader class of kernel functions.

</details>


### [98] [A note on the stability of Arens products under small perturbations of multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11379)
*Deepika Rajoriya*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究Banach代数乘法结构的小扰动对二次对偶上两个Arens乘积的影响，证明Arens不规则性在小扰动下稳定，并证明与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数的Arens正则性在乘法小扰动下稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arens乘积与Arens正则/不规则性是Banach代数二次对偶理论的核心概念，而代数结构在扰动下的稳定性是重要问题。本文旨在探究乘法结构的小扰动如何影响二次对偶上的Arens乘积及其正则性。

Method: 对Banach代数$\mathcal A$的乘法结构施加充分小的扰动，分析该扰动对二次对偶$\mathcal A^{**}$上两个Arens乘积的影响，并分别讨论Arens不规则性与Arens正则性的稳定性。对于Arens正则性，聚焦于与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数这一特殊类。

Result: 证明了Arens不规则性在乘法结构充分小扰动下保持稳定；并证明了对于任何与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数，其Arens正则性在原有Banach函数代数乘法结构的小扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: Banach代数乘法结构的微小扰动不会破坏Arens不规则性这一性质，且在与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数情形下Arens正则性同样具有小扰动稳定性，揭示了Arens正则性相关性质在代数结构扰动下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this short note, we study the small perturbations of multiplication structure of a Banach algebra $\mathcal A$, and its impact on the two Arens products on the bidual $\mathcal A^{**}$. We show that the property of being \say{Arens irregular} is stable under sufficiently small perturbation in the multiplication structure of a Banach algebra. Further we show that for any Banach function algebra which is equivalent to a uniform algebra, the Arens regularity is stable under small perturbation on the multiplication structure of the original Banach function algebra.

</details>


### [99] [Noncommutative commutant lifting, interpolation, and shift-invariant subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11410)
*Sibaprasad Barik,Samir Panja*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在非交换 Drury–Arveson 空间框架下建立 Sarason 型交换子提升定理，用于求解非交换行球上的 Nevanlinna–Pick 插值问题，并给出位移不变子空间的 Beurling–Lax–Halmos 型刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将算子理论中的交换子提升定理与插值理论推广到非交换多变量设定，统一并涵盖经典单变量结果。

Method: 非交换 Drury–Arveson 空间上的 Sarason 型交换子提升；结合膨胀理论（dilation theoretic）技术处理位移不变子空间。

Result: 解决了非交换行球上的 Nevanlinna–Pick 插值问题，并恢复开单位圆盘上的经典 Nevanlinna–Pick 插值定理；同时得到位移不变子空间的 Beurling–Lax–Halmos 型刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为算子理论中的交换子提升、插值和不变子空间理论提供了非交换多变量的统一框架。

Abstract: We establish a Sarason-type commutant lifting theorem in the framework of noncommutative Drury-Arveson space and apply it to solve the noncommutative Nevanlinna-Pick interpolation problem on the noncommutative row ball. In particular, it recovers the classical Nevanlinna-Pick interpolation theorem over the open unit disc. We also provide a Beurling-Lax-Halmos-type characterization of shift-invariant subspaces of noncommutative Drury-Arveson space using dilation theoretic technique.

</details>


### [100] [Sharp Volume-Based Bounds for BMO Functions on Compact Domains and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11467)
*Marin Mišur*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文为紧支撑BMO函数建立了尖锐的局部 $L^r$ 估计，将经典John-Nirenberg估计中的支撑"直径"依赖替换为"Lebesgue测度"（体积）依赖，更适用于细长、高偏心率区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典经John-Nirenberg不等式得到的界用支撑直径控制 $L^r$ 范数，但直径对细长、高偏心率区域并非合适量度；作者希望改用与体积相关的量，从而在几何退化情形获得更优估计。

Method: 证明依赖非增重排（non-increasing rearrangements）与Bennett-DeVore-Sharpley定理，且无需消失振荡（vanishing-oscillation）假设。

Result: 证明支撑直径依赖可替换为支撑的Lebesgue测度，得到尖锐局部 $L^r$ 界；并给出三类应用：管状邻域中的几何坍缩、Schrödinger算子的局部Cwikel-Lieb-Rozenblum型界、以及Calderón-Zygmund奇异积分局部作用的解耦（decoupling）界。

Conclusion: 该工作推广并改进了BMO的局部 $L^r$ 估计，为细长域上的几何与分析问题提供了以体积为尺度的工具，并在几何坍缩、谱理论和奇异积分中具有应用价值。

Abstract: We establish sharp localized $L^r$ bounds for compactly supported functions of bounded mean oscillation (BMO). Classical estimates obtained via the John-Nirenberg inequality bound the $L^r$ norm of such a function in terms of the diameter of its support. We show that the diameter dependence can be replaced by the Lebesgue measure of the support, the relevant quantity for thin, highly eccentric domains. The proof relies on non-increasing rearrangements and the Bennett-DeVore-Sharpley theorem, and requires no vanishing-oscillation hypothesis. We present physical and operator-theoretic applications: geometric collapse in tubular neighborhoods, a localized Cwikel-Lieb-Rozenblum bound for Schrödinger operators, and decoupling bounds for the localized action of Calderón-Zygmund singular integrals.

</details>


### [101] [Jacobians of BV homeomorphisms and their weak-* limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11502)
*Jakub Petr*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中，任何 $BV$ 同胚以及 $BV$ 同胚的弱-* 极限都不能改变 Jacobian 的符号：$J_f \ge 0$ 几乎处处或 $J_f \le 0$ 几乎处处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注有界变差（$BV$）映射的拓扑与测度性质之间的相容性。对 $BV$ 同胚而言，Jacobian 的符号对应局部取向（是否保向），这一问题与弱收敛下取向是否可能发生翻转密切相关，是几何测度论与非线性弹性中函数类稳定性问题的核心。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中考察 $BV$ 同胚及其弱-* 极限，利用 $BV$ 映射的迹、近似可微性与 Jacobian 的分布性质，分析 Jacobian 正负集合的测度，证明不可能同时存在测度为正的 $J_f>0$ 与 $J_f<0$ 部分。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中任何 $BV$ 同胚以及 $BV$ 同胚的弱-* 极限都不能改变 Jacobian 符号，即要么 $J_f \ge 0$ 几乎处处，要么 $J_f \le 0$ 几乎处处。

Conclusion: 结果表明 $BV$ 同胚及其弱-* 极限类的取向符号具有刚性：Jacobian 不能变号。该结论排除取向翻转型弱极限，为弱收敛下的取向保持性提供了判据。

Abstract: We prove that in $\mathbb{R}^3$ any $BV$ homeomorphism as well as the weak-* limit of $BV$ homeomorphisms cannot change the sign of the Jacobian, i.e., we have $J_f \geq 0$ almost everywhere or $J_f \leq 0$ almost everywhere.

</details>


### [102] [The Local Embedding Problem for Hardy Spaces of Dirichlet Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11560)
*Bonan Chen,Xiang Fang,Feng Guo,Shengzhao Hou,Yizhou Shao,Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Dirichlet 级数 Hardy 空间 \(\mathscr{H}^p\) 的局部嵌入问题，得到临界线 \(\operatorname{Re}s=1/2\) 上的维数无关迹不等式，并将其有限指数阈值精确刻画为 \(p\ge2\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 \(\mathscr{H}^p\) 的整体范数是否控制临界线上的局部 \(L^p\) 质量。此前仅知 \(p=2\) 及偶数 \(p=2k\) 成立，并曾猜测这些穷尽了 \(p>2\) 的有限正情形；本文要确定真实阈值。

Method: 转至对偶指数 \(q=p/(p-1)\in(1,2)\)，通过精确频率分解分离出单一共振 Euler 乘积项；用保持协方差的素因子替换把矩估计化为对数相关 Gauss 场，并用临界分枝随机游走界给出多尺度衰减；再用有限循环平方函数估计组装共振尺度，最后用 Hardy 商对偶把向量值界转化为临界线迹。

Result: 对每个 \(2<p<\infty\)，存在与 Dirichlet 多项式所依赖素变量数目和选择无关的常数 \(C_p<\infty\)，使得
\[
\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb R}\int_\theta^{\theta+1}|P(1/2+it)|^p\,dt\le C_p\|P\|_{\mathscr{H}^p}^p.
\]
结合 \(0<p<2\) 已知的反例，得到尖锐分类：局部嵌入性质恰好对 \(p\ge2\) 成立。

Conclusion: 真实阈值是 \(p=2\)，而非偶数整数性。对 \(1\le p<\infty\)，已知等价关系还给出共形不变半平面嵌入、反向局部 Carleson 测度转移以及所有特征零 Gordon–Hedenmalm 复合算子有界性等经典问题的同一尖锐阈值。

Abstract: We solve the local embedding problem for Hardy spaces of Dirichlet series, which is a dimension-free trace problem asking whether the global $\mathscr{H}^p$-norm controls local $L^p$-mass on the critical line $\operatorname{Re}s=1/2$. More precisely, for every $2<p<\infty$, there exists a constant $C_p<\infty$ such that every Dirichlet polynomial $P$ satisfies $$ \sup_{θ\in\mathbb{R}}\int_θ^{θ+1}\left|P\left(\frac12+it\right)\right|^p\,\mathrm{d}t\le C_p\left\lVert P\right\rVert_{\mathscr{H}^p}^{p}, $$ with $C_p$ independent of the number and choice of prime variables on which $P$ depends. Before the present work, the embedding was known at $p=2$ and, by taking integer powers, at the even exponents $p=2k$; it had been conjectured that these exhaust the finite positive cases above $2$. Together with the known failure for $0<p<2$, our theorem gives the sharp finite-exponent classification: the local embedding property holds exactly for $p\ge2$. Thus the true threshold is $p=2$, rather than even integrality.
  The proof passes to the dual exponent $q=p/(p-1)\in(1,2)$, where an exact frequency decomposition isolates a single resonant Euler-product term. A covariance-preserving replacement of the shared prime factors reduces the resulting moment estimate to a log-correlated Gaussian field, and a critical branching-random-walk bound supplies the required multiscale decay. A finite-cyclic square-function estimate assembles the resonant scales, and Hardy-quotient duality converts the resulting vector-valued bound into the critical-line trace. For $1\le p<\infty$, known equivalences give the same sharp threshold in several classical problems, including the conformally invariant half-plane embedding, the reverse local Carleson-measure transfer, and boundedness of all characteristic-zero Gordon--Hedenmalm composition operators.

</details>


### [103] [Completeness of constructible norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11782)
*Juan Rafael Acosta-Portilla,Armando Sánchez-Nungaray,Gerardo Ramos-Vázquez,Lizbeth Yolanda Garrido-Ramírez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究Banach空间上等价范数族模去共线关系后的商空间，证明其在Banach-Mazur型度量下是完备度量空间，并证明凸集间有界Lipschitz映射空间上可构造范数族的完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为等价范数族赋予合适的度量结构，并考察其完备性，从而刻画范数空间的整体几何性质。

Method: 对给定Banach空间，取等价范数族关于共线关系的商，引入Banach-Mazur型度量；随后针对有界Lipschitz映射构成的空间上的可构造范数族，采用类似度量并验证完备性。

Result: 证明等价范数族模共线关系在Banach-Mazur型度量下构成完备度量空间；同时证明凸集间有界Lipschitz映射空间上的可构造范数族亦完备。

Conclusion: 等价范数族在商度量意义下具备完备性，可构造范数族同样完备，为范数族空间的度量几何研究提供了基础性结论。

Abstract: In the present work we study for a given Banach space the quotient of the family of equivalent norms modulus the collinearity relationship and prove that under a Banach-Mazur type metric this family is a complete metric space, and we also prove the completeness of the family of constructible norms for the Banach space of bounded Lipschitzian mappings between convex sets of a Banach space.

</details>


### [104] [The critical Schatten exponent for the Berger-Coburn endpoint problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11853)
*Jani A. Virtanen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定 Toeplitz 算子 Schatten 类与 Fock 空间热变换在端点 t=1/4 处的精确对应关系：p=1 是使 T_g ∈ S_p 强制 g^{(1/4)} 有界的精确 Schatten 指数，并给出最优常数 2^n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berger 与 Coburn 证明 Fock 空间上 Toeplitz 算子 T_g 的有界性可控制热变换 g^{(t)}（1/4<t<1），并猜想端点 g^{(1/4)} 刻画有界性；Looi 通过构造有界 T_g 但 g^{(1/4)} 无界的反例否定了该猜想。本文旨在确定替代的精确刻画条件。

Method: 研究 T_g 属于 Schatten 类 S_p 时对 g^{(1/4)} 有界性的强制作用；分析 Berger–Coburn 迹公式中出现的迹类算子在 t↓1/4 时的迹范数发散但强收敛到 2^n J 的现象；利用 Weyl 平移 W_a、宇称算子 J 及 Baire 纲论证构造反例；并求 Berger–Coburn 估计的最优常数。

Result: 证明 p=1 是 T_g ∈ S_p 强制 g^{(1/4)} 有界的精确 Schatten 指数；得到 g^{(1/4)}(a)=2^n tr(T_g W_a J W_a^*) 及 ‖g^{(1/4)}‖_∞≤2^n‖T_g‖_{S_1}，且 2^n 最优；该界对 0<p≤1 的 T_g ∈ S_p 同样成立；对 p>1 不存在相应 S_p 估计（即使紧支光滑符号也不行）；Baire 纲论证表明存在使 T_g ∈ S_p（对所有 p>1）但 g^{(1/4)} 无界的容许符号，但非显式；同时确定 1/4<t≤1 时 Berger–Coburn 估计的最优常数。

Conclusion: p=1 是连接 Toeplitz 算子 Schatten 类与端点热变换有界性的临界指数，常数 2^n 最优；p>1 时不存在类似估计，端点猜想在 Schatten 框架下被精确修正。

Abstract: Berger and Coburn showed that boundedness of a Toeplitz operator $T_g$ on the Fock space controls the heat transform $g^{(t)}$ for $1/4<t<1$, and conjectured the endpoint $g^{(1/4)}$ characterizes boundedness. Looi recently disproved this by constructing a bounded $T_g$ with unbounded $g^{(1/4)}$. We show that $p=1$ is the exact Schatten exponent for which $T_g\in S_p$ forces $g^{(1/4)}$ to be bounded. The trace-class operators appearing in Berger and Coburn's trace formula have divergent trace norms as $t\downarrow1/4$, yet converge strongly to $2^n J$, and it follows that $$
  g^{(1/4)}(a)=2^n\operatorname{tr}
  \bigl(T_gW_aJW_a^*\bigr),\qquad
  \|g^{(1/4)}\|_\infty\leq2^n\norm{T_g}_{S_1}, $$ for every admissible symbol $g$ with $T_g$ trace class, where $J$ is the parity operator and $W_a$ is Weyl translation. We prove that $2^n$ is optimal, and the same bound holds for $T_g\in S_p$ with $0<p\leq1$. For $p>1$, no corresponding $S_p$ estimate is possible, even for compactly supported smooth symbols. Moreover, a Baire category argument shows there is an admissible symbol $g$ with $T_g\in S_p$ for every $p>1$ while $g^{(1/4)}$ is unbounded, though it yields no explicit symbol. We also determine the optimal constants in the Berger--Coburn estimates for $1/4<t\leq1$.

</details>


### [105] [Improved Discrete Dual $p$-Hardy and Weighted Discrete $p$- Birman Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11890)
*Bikram Das*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立带位移的一维广义离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式，并据此改进两类离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式及建立带幂权的离散 p-Birman 不等式和多变量对偶 p-Hardy 不等式，且常数均为最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有的离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式及其幂权推广形式，并获得最佳常数，同时推广到多变量情形。

Method: 首先建立带位移的一维广义离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式；利用该不等式改进两类离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式；建立带幂权的离散 p-Birman 不等式；先对两个变量证明多变量对偶 p-Hardy 不等式，再推广到多个变量并保持常数最佳性。

Result: 对 p>1 且 A∈C_c(N_0)、A_0=A_1=0，改进了离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式（常数 1/p^p 最佳），并改进了其幂权推广（p-1<α≤p，常数 ((α+1-p)/p)^p 最佳）；同时建立带幂权的离散 p-Birman 不等式与具有最佳常数的多变量对偶 p-Hardy 不等式。

Conclusion: 带位移的广义离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式是改进经典离散对偶 p-Hardy 不等式及其幂权形式的有力工具，相关结果可推广至多变量情形且保持常数最佳性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a new version of one dimensional generalized discrete dual $p$-Hardy inequality with a shift. Using this generalized discrete dual p-Hardy inequality, we obtain improvements of two discrete dual $p$-Hardy inequalities. To be specific, for $p>1$ and $A\in C_c(\mathbb{N}_{0})$ satisfying $A_{0}=A_{1}=0$, we first improve the discrete dual p-Hardy inequality \begin{align*} &\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(n-1)^{p}| A_{n}-A_{n-1}|^{p}\geq\frac{1}{p^{p}}\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|A_{n}|^{p}, \end{align*} where the associate constant term is sharp. Subsequently, we improve its power-type weighted discrete dual p-Hardy extension \begin{align*} &\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(n-1)^α|A_{n}-A_{n-1}|^{p}\geq\Big(\frac{α+1-p}{p}\Big)^{p} \displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{|A_{n}|^{p}}{n^{p-α}} \end{align*} for $p-1<α\leq p$, where the associated constant term is also sharp. We also establish a discrete $p$- Birman inequality with power weights. Furthermore, we establish a multivariable dual $p$-Hardy inequality with a sharp constant. The proof proceeds by first establishing the inequality for two variables and then extending the argument to multiple variables, while preserving the sharpness of the constant.

</details>


### [106] [Upper bound properties of free and related Banach lattices via operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11928)
*Anastasiia Ianina,Timur Oikhberg*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入与映入 $X$ 的算子相关的新工具，研究 Banach 空间 $X$ 上的自由 $p$-凸 Banach 格 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 及其相关格的 Fatou 性质、单调有界性与嵌入正则性，并建立 Fatou 型性质与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由 $p$-凸 Banach 格 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 可表示为 $X^*$ 单位球上的函数空间，由此产生若干相关的（更大的）格。对这些格的 Fatou 性质、单调有界性以及空间之间嵌入的正则性缺乏有效的研究工具，因此需要引入新方法加以刻画，并揭示其与 $X$ 本身几何性质（如 Radon-Nikodym 性质）的关联。

Method: 引入一个与映入 $X$ 的算子相关的新工具，用于分析 $X^*$ 单位球上函数所构成的相关格的结构。利用该工具：(i) 判定所讨论的格是否具有涉及上定向集上确界的性质，即 Fatou 性质及相关的单调有界性；(ii) 考察相关空间之间嵌入的正则性。此外，构造反例说明存在 $X \subset Y$ 使得 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 不是 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$ 的正则子格。

Result: 建立了 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 的 Fatou 型性质与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质之间的联系；给出了 $X \subset Y$ 的例子，使得 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 不是 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$ 的正则子格。

Conclusion: 所引入的算子工具能有效判定 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 及相关格的 Fatou 性质与单调有界性，并刻画嵌入的正则性；Fatou 型性质与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质密切相关，且嵌入未必保持正则子格结构。

Abstract: It is known that the free $p$-convex Banach lattice on a Banach space $X$ can be represented as a space of functions on the unit ball of $X^*$. In this way, it gives rise to certain related (larger) lattices. To investigate such lattices, we introduce a new tool, related to operators into $X$. This tool is then used to (i) determine whether the lattices in question possess, or fail, properties involving upper bounds of upward directed sets -- namely, the Fatou property, and the related property of monotonic boundedness; (ii) investigate the regularity of embeddings between spaces in question. We find connections between the Fatou-like properties of ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ and the Radon-Nikodym property of $X$. In addition, we give an example of $X \subset Y$ such that ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ is not a regular sublattice of ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Rigidity and flexibility under positive isotropic curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11702)
*Tsz-Kiu Aaron Chow,Yipeng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文否定了正迷向曲率下Urysohn 1-宽度与稳定极小盘半径的猜想界，并证明了闭偶维流形在σ-PIC条件下关于λ₁⁽²⁾的 sharp 估计及等号刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献曾猜想，在迷向曲率有正下界时，Urysohn 1-宽度与嵌入稳定极小盘的内半径应受曲率下界控制。作者旨在检验并推翻这些宽度与稳定盘半径界。

Method: 对每个 n≥4 和 L>0，在 S^n 上构造光滑 4-PIC 度量；同时研究闭偶维流形在 σ-PIC 下的第一非零 L²-调和 2-形式特征值 λ₁⁽²⁾，并分析达到等号的闭特征形式的秩条件。

Result: 构造出的度量使 Urysohn 1-宽度至少为 L，且含内半径至少为 L 的嵌入稳定极小盘，从而否定原猜想界。另一方面证明 sharp 估计 λ₁⁽²⁾≥(n-1)σ/2；若某达到等号的闭特征形式在一点秩至少为四，则度量必为圆度量。

Conclusion: 正下界迷向曲率并不能控制 Urysohn 1-宽度或稳定极小盘半径；但在闭偶维流形上，σ-PIC 给出 λ₁⁽²⁾ 的 sharp 下界，并在秩≥4 的等号情形下蕴含圆刚性。

Abstract: For every $n\ge4$ and $L>0$, we construct a smooth $4$-PIC metric on $S^n$ with Urysohn $1$-width at least $L$ and an embedded stable minimal disk of intrinsic inradius at least $L$. These examples disprove the proposed width and stable-disk radius bounds under a positive lower bound for isotropic curvature. On closed even-dimensional manifolds, we prove the sharp estimate $λ_1^{(2)}\ge(n-1)σ/2$ under $σ$-PIC and show that equality forces roundness if a closed eigenform attaining the bound has rank at least four at some point.

</details>


### [108] [Twistor theory of Loxodromes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10761)
*Maciej Dunajski,Wojciech Kryński*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用扭量对应（twistor correspondence）刻画了 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中与 $S^4$ 上斜驶线（loxodromes）对应的 $14$ 维齐次直纹曲面族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭量对应建立了 $S^4$ 中曲线与 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中直纹曲面之间的对偶关系，前者共形性质反映为后者射影性质。本文旨在借助此对偶刻画 $S^4$ 上斜驶线对应的直纹曲面结构。

Method: 运用扭量对应这一对偶工具，将 $S^4$ 上斜驶线的共形几何翻译为 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中直纹曲面的射影几何，进而刻画对应的齐次直纹曲面族。

Result: 刻画了一个 $14$ 维齐次直纹曲面族（位于 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中），该族恰好对应于 $S^4$ 上的斜驶线。

Conclusion: 扭量对应为研究 $S^4$ 曲线的共形性质与其在 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中对应直纹曲面射影性质之间的关系提供了有效框架，并成功用于描述斜驶线对应的齐次直纹曲面族。

Abstract: The twistor correspondence provides a duality between curves in $S^4$ and ruled surfaces in $\mathbb{CP}^3$ in which the conformal properties of the former are reflected in the projective properties of the latter. We use this to characterise a $14$--dimensional family of homogeneous ruled surfaces in $\mathbb{CP}^3$ which correspond to loxodromes in $S^4$.

</details>


### [109] [The $q$-deformed cross-ratio: modular invariants and Coxeter friezes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10999)
*Valentin Ovsienko*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了 $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$ 上交比的标量 $q$-形变，基于 $q$-形变有理数概念构造 $q$-交比，其与 $q$-形变 Coxeter 镶边相关，并在 $q=e^h$ 展开中得到模不变量序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在交比这一经典射影几何不变量上引入 $q$-形变，从而联系 $q$-形变有理数、$\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb Z)$ 作用以及 Coxeter 镶边等结构，并探索其展开所产生的模不变量。

Method: 基于 Morier-Genoud 与作者的 $q$-形变有理数概念，构造 $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$ 上的标量 $q$-交比；研究其在 $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb Z)$ 下的不变性及 $\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb Z)$ 中行列式为 $-1$ 元素作用下 $q\mapsto q^{-1}$ 的变换；并将其与有理多边形对应的 $q$-形变 Coxeter 镶边建立联系；随后在 $q=e^h$ 处展开，计算前两个非平凡系数。

Result: $q$-交比在 $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb Z)$ 下不变，$\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb Z)$ 中行列式为 $-1$ 的元素作用为 $q\mapsto q^{-1}$；其与有理多边形的 $q$-形变 Coxeter 镶边相关；在 $q=e^h$ 展开中得到一列代数独立的模不变量与相对不变量，但该序列不能区分模轨道；作者显式算出了展开的前两个非平凡系数。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了交比的 $q$-形变框架，揭示了其与 $q$-形变有理数及 Coxeter 镶边的联系，并提供了一列由展开产生的模不变量，尽管该序列不足以分离模轨道。

Abstract: We introduce and study a scalar $q$-deformation of the cross-ratio on $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$. Our construction is based on the notion of $q$-deformed rational numbers due to Morier-Genoud and the author. The $q$-cross-ratio is invariant under $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$, while elements of determinant $-1$ of $\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ act by $q\mapsto q^{-1}$. A principal result is its relation to $q$-deformed Coxeter friezes associated with rational polygons. The expansion at $q=e^h$ yields an algebraically independent sequence of modular invariants and relative invariants, although this sequence does not separate modular orbits. We compute the first two nonconstant coefficients of this expansion explicitly.

</details>


### [110] [The Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in dimension five](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11005)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了在非正截面曲率的完备单连通Riemann 5-流形中，欧氏等周不等式（尖锐形式）成立，从而在该维度解决了Cartan-Hadamard猜想；核心是借助Banchoff-Pohl风格的边界点对积分与Jacobi场估计，证明常平均曲率超曲面的尖锐不等式，并结合Kleiner的等周剖面论证完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard猜想是等周几何中的核心问题：在非正截面曲率的完备单连通流形中，欧氏空间的等周不等式应成立。该猜想长期仅在低维及部分情形已知，本文旨在解决5维情形，并给出3维的新证明。

Method: 主步骤是证明常平均曲率超曲面的尖锐不等式，证明使用类似Banchoff-Pohl的边界点对积分方法，并结合沿测地弦的Jacobi场估计；进一步证明该不等式对测地球中等周区域的边界（仅在自由部分平均曲率恒定）仍成立；最后采用Kleiner的等周剖面论证完成整体证明。

Result: 在非正截面曲率的完备单连通Riemann 5-流形中，尖锐欧氏等周不等式成立，即在该维度确立了Cartan-Hadamard猜想；同时所给方法为3维情形提供了新的证明。

Conclusion: Cartan-Hadamard猜想在5维得到解决，方法可推广并重新证明3维情形，表明基于边界点对积分、Jacobi场估计与等周剖面论证的框架是处理此类尖锐等周问题的有效途径。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $5$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in that dimension. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, in the spirit of Banchoff-Pohl, together with an estimate for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part. An isoperimetric-profile argument, after Kleiner, completes the proof. Our method also gives a new proof in dimension $3$.

</details>


### [111] [On static manifolds with boundary admitting a nowhere-vanishing static potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11349)
*Vladimir Medvedev*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究带边界完备静态流形（存在无处消失静态势）。在标量曲率与边界平均曲率的自然下界条件下，证明简单带边界静态流形必具有正标量曲率、负边界平均曲率且紧致；给出体积与边界几何的显式关系与估计。标量平坦情形下证明整体分裂与Ricci平坦刚性（含非连通边界）；负标量曲率情形下建立尖锐平均曲率界并以指数warped-product结构刻画等号情形。证明核心依赖于相关Einstein流形的研究，附录给出带边界静态流形的恒等式并讨论无边情形下的Einstein流形技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带边界静态流形在广义相对论与几何分析中具有基本意义，但其在边界存在时的整体刚性、紧致性与几何估计尚缺乏系统刻画。作者旨在借助相关Einstein流形技术，在标量曲率与边界平均曲率的自然下界假设下，建立带边界静态流形的刚性、分裂与等号刻画。

Method: 核心方法是研究相关联的Einstein流形（associated Einstein manifold），将带边界静态流形问题转化为Einstein流形问题；附录中推导带边界静态流形的若干恒等式，并在无边情形下讨论该Einstein流形技巧。

Result: 在自然下界条件下，简单带边界静态流形必具正标量曲率、负边界平均曲率且紧致，并获得体积与边界几何的显式关系与估计；标量平坦情形得到整体分裂与Ricci平坦刚性（含非连通边界）；负标量曲率情形得到尖锐平均曲率界，并以指数warped-product结构刻画等号情形。

Conclusion: 带边界静态流形在自然曲率下界下呈现强刚性：紧致性、符号确定以及分裂/刚性结构均可由相关Einstein流形方法统一导出，负标量曲率情形的平均曲率界为尖锐且等号情形具指数warped-product结构。

Abstract: We study complete static manifolds with boundary admitting a nowhere-vanishing static potential. Our main result shows that, under a natural lower bound relating the scalar curvature and the boundary mean curvature, a simple static manifold with boundary must in fact have positive scalar curvature, negative boundary mean curvature, and be compact; we also obtain explicit relations and estimates involving the volume of the manifold and the geometry of its boundary. In the scalar-flat case, we prove global splitting and Ricci-flat rigidity results, including for disconnected boundary, while in the negative scalar curvature case we establish a sharp mean-curvature bound and characterize the equality case by an exponential warped-product structure. The proofs rely essentially on the study of the associated Einstein manifold. In appendix we derive several identities for static manifolds with boundary and discuss the associated Einstein manifold technique in the boundaryless setting.

</details>


### [112] [Non-strict negativity of holomorphic bisectional curvature for compact relative Kähler fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11388)
*Xueyuan Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否定了To-Yeung提出的开问题：紧全纯纤维化的纤维与底空间上严格负全纯双截面曲率并不必然传递到全空间，即使Kodaira-Spencer映射处处单射。作者构造了亏格2曲线上相对Kähler纤维化（全空间为光滑射影三维簇），纤维度量严格负双截面曲率、KS映射处处单射，但全空间不容许此类曲率度量；并构造了有效参数化的Monge-Ampère纤维化，其广义Weil-Petersson度量双截面非正、全纯截面严格负，但混合双截面曲率消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全纯纤维化中曲率正性/负性从底空间与纤维到全空间的传递问题。To与Yeung提出开问题：当KS映射处处单射时，纤维与底空间上的严格负全纯双截面曲率是否传递到全空间。本文旨在给出反例并深入理解该传递失效现象及参数空间自然度量的曲率行为。

Method: 构造亏格2曲线上的紧相对Kähler纤维化，全空间为光滑射影三维簇，且Kodaira-Spencer映射处处单射，诱导纤维度量具严格负全纯双截面曲率，但全空间无此类度量。另构造来自Shimura曲线上通用四元数阿贝尔曲面的紧、有效参数化Monge-Ampère纤维化。对其乘积族计算广义Weil-Petersson度量的曲率。

Result: 得到KS映射处处单射情形下的相对Kähler纤维化反例，证明全空间不容许严格负全纯双截面曲率度量，从而否定To-Yeung开问题。对Monge-Ampère纤维化乘积族，广义Weil-Petersson度量具非正全纯双截面曲率与严格负全纯截面曲率，但混合双截面曲率恒为零，尽管每点都有效变化。

Conclusion: 严格双截面负性在两方面均可能失效：(1)全空间度量的存在性；(2)参数空间自然（Weil-Petersson）度量的混合双截面曲率，即使每点有效变化。表明曲率传递并非普遍成立，回答了To-Yeung问题。

Abstract: Strict negativity of holomorphic bisectional curvature need not pass from the base and fibers of a compact holomorphic fibration to its total space, even when the Kodaira-Spencer map is everywhere injective. We construct a compact relative Kähler fibration over a genus-two curve, with a smooth projective threefold as total space and an everywhere injective Kodaira--Spencer map, whose induced fiber metrics have strictly negative holomorphic bisectional curvature, whereas the total space admits no Kähler metric with this curvature property. This gives a negative answer to an open problem posed by To and Yeung. We also construct compact, effectively parametrized Monge-Ampère fibrations from universal quaternionic abelian surfaces over Shimura curves. For products of these families, the generalized Weil-Petersson metric has nonpositive holomorphic bisectional curvature and strictly negative holomorphic sectional curvature, but its mixed bisectional curvatures vanish. Thus strict bisectional negativity can fail both for the existence of a metric on the total space and for the natural metric on the parameter space, despite effective variation at every point.

</details>


### [113] [Optimal geometric inequalities and fully nonlinear conformal flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11421)
*Yuxin Ge,Guofang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在球面上建立涉及总σ_k-曲率∫σ_k(g)dv_g的尖锐Sobolev型几何不等式，将Guan–Wang的最优不等式从锥C_k推广到更大的锥C_{k-1}，并给出维数3、4下两个公开问题的解答，同时举例说明不能推广到C_{k-2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guan–Wang先前已在锥C_k上得到最优不等式，但其允许的共形度量范围受限。本文旨在扩大可容许共形度量的范围，将结果推广到严格更大的锥C_{k-1}，从而覆盖更一般的共形类。

Method: 采用变分方法，通过一个完全非线性共形流实现。在C_{k-1}中工作带来显著解析困难，需要在验证流保持抛物性的同时获得C^2先验估计；作者通过精心设计的测试函数以及将最大值原理应用于Hessian矩阵的最大特征值来解决这些问题。

Result: 在S^n上建立了涉及总σ_k-曲率的尖锐Sobolev型几何不等式，把Guan–Wang的最优不等式从C_k推广到C_{k-1}。作为应用，解决了维数3和4中的两个公开问题；并举例表明这些不等式无法推广到C_{k-2}。

Conclusion: 该工作扩大了最优Sobolev型几何不等式的适用范围，给出了最优性不能进一步推广的边界（至C_{k-2}即失效），并解决了两个公开问题。

Abstract: We establish sharp Sobolev-type geometric inequalities on $\mathbb{S}^n$ involving the total $σ_k$-curvatures $\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}σ_k(g)\,dv_g$. These results extend the optimal inequalities of Guan--Wang~\cite{GWDuke} from the cone $\mathcal{C}_k$ to the strictly larger cone $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$, thereby enlarging the range of admissible conformal metrics. Our approach is variational and is implemented through a fully nonlinear conformal flow. Working in $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$ introduces substantial analytic difficulties; in particular, one must obtain $C^2$ a priori estimates while simultaneously verifying that the flow remains parabolic. We resolve these issues via a carefully designed test function and by applying the maximum principle to the maximal eigenvalue of the Hessian matrix. As applications, we solve two open problems in dimensions 3 and 4. Finally, we give examples to show that these inequalities cannot be extended to $\mathcal{C}_{k-2}$.

</details>


### [114] [A Seifert Dichotomy for Z2-Harmonic One-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11429)
*Weifeng Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面上的Z/2-调和1-形式建立了二分性结果：当例外纤维数至多为3时，在纤维充分塌缩的联络-度量族下不存在；当例外纤维数至少为4时，任意黎曼度量下均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画Z/2-调和1-形式在Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面上的存在性条件，厘清例外纤维数目与度量塌缩程度对存在性的影响，并补齐非可定向基的相应分类。

Method: 研究对象为Z/2-调和1-形式与Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面。针对至多三个例外纤维的可定向基，沿固定联络-度量族考察纤维充分塌缩情形证明不存在性；对至少四个例外纤维引用已有存在性结果；并对非可定向基完成分类。

Result: 得到二分性结论：至多三个例外纤维、可定向基时，在纤维充分塌缩的联络-度量族中不存在Z/2-调和1-形式；至少四个例外纤维时，任意黎曼度量下均存在；同时给出非可定向基的完整分类。

Conclusion: Z/2-调和1-形式在Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面上的存在性由例外纤维数目决定，呈现明确的二分性，且该分类可推广至非可定向基情形。

Abstract: We establish a dichotomy for Z/2-harmonic one-forms on Seifert fibered rational homology three-spheres. For an oriented base with at most three exceptional fibers, we prove nonexistence along any fixed connection-metric family when the fibers are sufficiently collapsed. With at least four exceptional fibers, existing results give existence for every Riemannian metric. We also complete the corresponding classification for nonorientable bases.

</details>


### [115] [Stability and Area-Minimizing Property of Higher-Dimensional Helicoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11481)
*Chung-Jun Tsai,Mao-Pei Tsui,Jingbo Wan,Mu-Tao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究高维螺旋面族 $H_k\\subset\\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ 的稳定性与极小面积性质，完全确定其稳定性：$k\\geq 3$ 稳定、$k\\leq 2$ 不稳定；并对 $k\\geq 3$ 确定面积最小性（$k$ 偶时成立，奇时不成立）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类螺旋面是完全、真嵌入、微分同胚于 $\\mathbb{R}^{k+1}$ 的极小子流形中基础而重要的族，是经典 $\\mathbb{R}^3$ 螺旋面的高维自然推广。此前对其稳定性与极小面积性质缺乏完整刻画，作者希望确定其稳定性相变及面积最小性规律。

Method: 通过研究参数化 $(u_1,\\ldots,u_k,s)\\mapsto(u_1e^{is},\\ldots,u_ke^{is},s)$ 定义的 $(k+1)$ 维螺旋面进行稳定性分析；对 $k\\geq 3$ 情形，用构造显式校准（calibration）证明面积最小性，用显式竞争（competitor）证明非面积最小性。

Result: 稳定性：$H_k$ 对 $k\\geq 3$ 稳定，对 $k\\leq 2$ 不稳定，在 $k=3$ 处发生尖锐相变（$k=1$ 经典螺旋面与 $k=2$ 高维类比均不稳定，而四维 $H_3\\subset\\mathbb{R}^7$ 已稳定）。面积最小性（限 $k\\geq 3$）：$k$ 为偶数时 $H_k$ 面积最小，$k$ 为奇数时不面积最小。特别地，对每个偶数 $k\\geq 4$，$H_k$ 是 $\\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ 中面积最小的完整极小图。

Conclusion: 高维螺旋面的稳定性与面积最小性由维数（$k$ 的奇偶与阈值）决定，$k=3$ 是稳定性由不稳定转向稳定的临界点，偶数 $k$ 时螺旋面作为完整极小图具有面积最小性。

Abstract: For each integer $k\geq 1$, we study the $(k+1)$-dimensional helicoid $H_k\subset\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ parametrized by \[
  (u_1,\ldots,u_k,s) \longmapsto \bigl(u_1e^{is},\ldots,u_ke^{is},s\bigr) \in \mathbb{C}^k\times\mathbb{R}\cong \mathbb{R}^{2k+1}. \] These helicoids form a basic and distinguished family of complete, properly embedded minimal submanifolds diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^{k+1}$, and provide natural higher-dimensional analogues of the classical helicoid in $\mathbb{R}^3$.
  We completely determine their stability: $H_k$ is stable for $k\geq 3$ and unstable for $k\leq 2$. The sharp transition at $k=3$ is particularly striking: while the classical helicoid $(k=1)$ and its first higher-dimensional analogue $(k=2)$ are unstable, the four-dimensional helicoid $H_3\subset\mathbb{R}^7$ is already stable.
  For $k\geq 3$, we also determine their area-minimizing property: $H_k$ is area-minimizing when $k$ is even and not area-minimizing when $k$ is odd. The area-minimizing result is proved by constructing an explicit calibration, while the non-area-minimizing result follows from an explicit competitor. In particular, for every even $k\geq 4$, the $(k+1)$-dimensional helicoid $H_k$ is an entire minimal graph in $\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ that is area-minimizing.

</details>


### [116] [A positively curved metric on the Gromoll-Meyer sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11484)
*Zexuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Sp(2)双不变度量的显式形变，使其在Gromoll-Meyer奇异7球面上诱导的度量在充分小正参数下具有正截面曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇异7球面上是否存在正截面曲率度量是黎曼几何中的重要问题，本文旨在为Gromoll-Meyer奇异7球面构造正截面曲率度量。

Method: 通过一个与商作用相容的固定自伴算子构造Sp(2)双不变度量的显式形变；结合一致曲率估计与水平交换平面的代数分析，在低阶正项消失的临界集上建立曲率分子三次系数的显式正下界，并对四阶余项进行一致控制，最后用紧性论证。

Result: 证明在充分小的正参数下，诱导度量在Gromoll-Meyer奇异7球面上的所有截面曲率均为正。

Conclusion: Gromoll-Meyer奇异7球面容许正截面曲率度量。

Abstract: We construct an explicit deformation of the bi-invariant metric on Sp(2) whose induced metrics on the Gromoll-Meyer exotic 7-sphere have positive sectional curvature for all sufficiently small positive parameters. The deformation is determined by a fixed self-adjoint operator compatible with the quotient action. The proof combines a uniform curvature estimate with an algebraic analysis of the horizontal commuting planes. On the critical set where the lower-order positive terms vanish, we establish an explicit positive lower bound for the cubic coefficient of the curvature numerator. Uniform control of the fourth-order remainder and a compactness argument then show that every sectional curvature is positive

</details>


### [117] [A Complex Structure on A Rational Homology Six-Sphere with Two-Torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11541)
*Zichang Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造了一个紧致、光滑、单连通的复三维流形，其第二、第三整 homology 群均为二阶循环群，其余同调群与六维球面一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复几何与代数拓扑中，寻找与拓扑球面同调特征相似但具有复结构的流形一直是重要议题。六维球面为单连通且同调极简（仅 H0 与 H6 非零），本文希望通过构造复三维流形探究复结构对其同调群的影响，并检验是否存在与球面高度同调、但仍保有低阶挠同调的例子。

Method: 摘要未提供具体构造细节，仅说明通过某种构造手段（可能涉及代数几何或复流形手术等方法）得到该复三维流形。

Result: 得到紧致、光滑、单连通的复三维流形；其二阶及三阶整同调群为 Z/2Z，其余同调群与六维球面相同。

Conclusion: 存在一个具有复结构的六维流形，其同调仅与六维球面在同调上相差两个二阶挠群，说明球面的拓扑容许与复流形结构相容的非平凡挠同调。

Abstract: We construct a compact, smooth, simply connected complex threefold. Its second and third integral homology groups are cyclic of order two, and its remaining homology groups agree with those of the six-sphere.

</details>


### [118] [Non-trivial ABC-Massey products on complex parallelisable solvmanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11597)
*Nunzia Cesarino,Tommaso Sferruzza,Adriano Tomassini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明每个非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数都存在非消没的三重ABC-Massey积，并由此推出每个紧复平行可解流形也具有此性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三重ABC-Massey积是紧复流形Bott-Chern与Aeppli上同调上的高阶运算，其非消没性是多元势同伦型不变量。作者希望刻画哪些复流形携带此类非平凡高阶运算，特别是可解李代数与可解流形的情形。

Method: 在非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数上直接构造并计算三重ABC-Massey积；随后利用紧复平行可解流形作为可解李代数的商这一对应关系，将李代数层面的结果提升到流形层面。

Result: 证明了任意非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数均具有非消没的三重ABC-Massey积；进而得到任意紧复平行可解流形也都具有非消没的三重ABC-Massey积。

Conclusion: 非消没三重ABC-Massey积在非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数及其对应的紧复平行可解流形中是普遍存在的，表明此类高阶运算在可解几何中具有结构性的非平凡性。

Abstract: Triple Aeppli--Bott--Chern--Massey products, shortly, triple ABC-Massey products are higher-order operations on the Bott--Chern and Aeppli cohomologies of a compact complex manifold, and their non-vanishing is an invariant of its pluripotential homotopy type. We prove that every non-Abelian complex unimodular solvable Lie algebra admits a non-vanishing triple ABC--Massey product, and we deduce that so does every compact complex parallelisable solvmanifold.

</details>


### [119] [Spectral complexes and currents on the Engel group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11625)
*Filippa Lo Biundo,Francesca Tripaldi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究Engel群上de Rham复形作为截断多重复形产生的谱复形，刻画了两个复形及其紧支对偶，并证明了Pansu拉回诱导谱流复形态射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在次黎曼几何中，Engel群是典型的非交换非紧李群，其上de Rham复形具有截断多重复形结构，然而其谱复形、紧支版本及流对偶性质尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其是Pansu拉回在低正则性下的上同调行为需要明确。

Method: 将Engel群G上的de Rham复形视为截断多重复形，由此构造两个谱复形；分析其紧支对应物，并通过流对偶定义谱流；证明光滑谱复形自然嵌入流复形；对W^{1,q}_{loc}映射φ:G→G（q>7）考察其Pansu拉回，证明其诱导谱流复形之间的态射。

Result: 刻画了Engel群上由de Rham复形产生的两个谱复形及其紧支对偶形式；定义了相应的谱流；证明了光滑谱复形到流复形的自然嵌入；并证明当q>7时，W^{1,q}_{loc}映射的Pansu拉回诱导谱流复形之间的态射。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Engel群上谱复形与谱流的对偶框架，为在低正则性映射下研究次黎曼流形的上同调拉回提供了工具。

Abstract: We study the spectral complexes arising from the de Rham complex on the Engel group $G$ viewed as a truncated multicomplex. We describe the two resulting complexes, analyse their compactly supported counterparts, and define the corresponding spectral currents by duality. We prove that the smooth spectral complexes embed naturally into the current complexes. Finally, we show that the Pansu pullback of a $W_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,q}$ map $\varphi\colon G\to G$ with $q>7$ induces a morphism into the corresponding complexes of spectral currents.

</details>


### [120] [A Thorpe Trick for the Bochner Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11653)
*Matthias Wink*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：当 n≥6 时，存在 ε(n)>0，使得任何具有 (⌈n/2⌉+ε)-正曲率算子的闭可定向黎曼流形都是实同调球；并证明 n=6 时 4-正曲率算子情形同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲率算子的部分正性（k-正曲率算子）如何约束闭可定向黎曼流形的拓扑，特别是将曲率正性阈值与实同调球这一拓扑结论联系起来，逼近已知刚性/分类结果的临界情形。

Method: 采用曲率算子的 k-正性（即曲率算子任意 k 个最小特征值之和为正）这一框架，结合（可能是 Bochner 型）曲率与调和形式/同调的消没论证或流形分类技术，对 n≥6 的一般情形证明存在 ε(n)>0，并对 n=6 单独处理至 4-正情形。

Result: 对 n≥6，存在 ε(n)>0，使 (⌈n/2⌉+ε)-正曲率算子的闭可定向黎曼流形为实同调球；n=6 时该结论在 4-正曲率算子条件下成立。

Conclusion: 曲率算子的正性阈值可提升至略高于 ⌈n/2⌉（n≥6）或等于 4（n=6）时，流形必为实同调球，给出了一类曲率刚性结果及最优/接近最优的阈值刻画。

Abstract: We prove that for $n \geq 6$ there exists $\varepsilon (n)>0$ such that every closed orientable Riemannian manifold with $\left( \lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil+ \varepsilon\right)$-positive curvature operator is a real homology sphere. For $n=6$ we show that the same result holds for manifolds with $4$-positive curvature operators.

</details>


### [121] [A vanishing result for harmonic $(n-1,1)$-forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11665)
*Matthias Wink*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明在Kähler曲率算子满足特定正性条件下，闭Kähler流形上的本原调和(n-1,1)-形式必为零，并由此推出3-正Kähler曲率算子的复维数≥3闭Kähler流形实上同调为复射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kähler曲率算子正性与调和形式消没性之间的联系，推广经典消没定理，并为闭Kähler流形的刚性分类提供判据。

Method: 利用Kähler曲率算子的正性假设，对本原调和(n-1,1)-形式应用Bochner型积分消没技术（Weitzenböck公式/曲率项估计），分复维数n=3与n≥4两种情形给出相应的正性阈值。

Result: 证得：复维数n≥4的闭Kähler流形，若Kähler曲率算子((n^2-n+2)/2)-正，则其任意本原调和(n-1,1)-形式为零；n=3时相应阈值为7/2-正。

Conclusion: 作为推论，复维数n≥3且Kähler曲率算子3-正的闭Kähler流形，其实上同调同构于复射影空间，即该流形为实上同调复射影空间。

Abstract: We prove that every primitive harmonic $(n-1,1)$-form of a closed Kähler manifold of complex dimension $n$ vanishes provided its Kähler curvature operator is $\frac{n^2-n+2}{2}$-positive if $n \geq 4$, respectively $\frac{7}{2}$-positive if $n=3$. As a consequence, a closed Kähler manifold of complex dimension $n \geq 3$ with $3$-positive Kähler curvature operator is a real cohomology complex projective space.

</details>


### [122] [The Rigidity of the closed three dimensional regular Landsberg metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11718)
*Jianyu Mao,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在闭三维流形上证明了Landsberg-Berwald猜想：任何光滑强凸正则Landsberg度量必为Berwald度量，从而不存在Landsberg"独角兽"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landsberg-Berwald猜想长期悬而未决，即是否每个正则Landsberg度量都是Berwald度量。该问题在Finsler几何中具有重要意义，且一般情形尚未解决，因此需要针对特定维数取得突破。

Method: 证明综合三项工具：闭曲面上交换Codazzi三次张量的消没定理；具有常曲率指标面的三维Minkowski范数的刚性定理；非线性曲率的秩一论证。剩余的R-二次情形通过沿测地流的紧性论证解决。

Result: 在闭三维流形上，每个光滑强凸正则Landsberg度量均为Berwald度量，且无需任何可逆性假设；即闭三维流形上不存在正则Landsberg"独角兽"。

Conclusion: 该猜想在闭三维流形情形下获得解决。论文澄清："独角兽不存在"仅是同一猜想的重述而非独立猜想；纤维化常曲率定理是论证中的工具，并不断言完整的Laugwitz猜想。

Abstract: The Landsberg Berwald conjecture asks whether every regular Landsberg metric is Berwald. We settle this conjecture for closed three-dimensional manifolds: every smooth strongly convex regular Landsberg metric on such a manifold is Berwald, without any reversibility assumption. Equivalently, there are no regular Landsberg "unicorns" on closed three-manifolds; this is a reformulation of the same conjecture, rather than a second independent conjecture.The proof combines a vanishing theorem for commuting Codazzi cubic tensors on closed surfaces, a rigidity theorem for three dimensional Minkowski norms with constant curvature indicatrices, and a rank one argument for the nonlinear curvature. The remaining R-quadratic case is settled by compactness along the geodesic flow. The fibrewise constant curvature theorem is an ingredient in this argument and does not assert the full Laugwitz conjecture.

</details>


### [123] [Local classification of chsc Kähler metrics with cone singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11760)
*Martin de Borbon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究锥奇点附近常全纯截面曲率Kähler度量的刚性问题，证明了在适当的全纯坐标下，该度量是复空间形式经幂映射的拉回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复几何中，锥奇点附近具有常全纯截面曲率(chsc)的Kähler度量的局部结构是一个核心问题。已有工作多针对模型锥度量，而对其余与该模型度量一致等价、沿奇点集多齐次的更一般度量，其刚性（即是否必为复空间形式的拉回）尚未明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，刻画此类度量的唯一性形态。

Method: 设定球 $B\subset\mathbb{C}^n$，奇点集 $D=\{z^1=0\}$。考察 $B\setminus D$ 上常全纯截面曲率的Kähler度量 $g$，要求其与角度 $2\pi\beta$（$0<\beta<1$）的模型锥度量一致等价，且沿 $D$ 多齐次。通过构造适当的、以原点为中心的全纯坐标 $(w^1,\dots,w^n)$，分析度量在锥奇点处的渐近展开与刚性，证明 $g$ 可表示为复空间形式经幂映射 $\bigl((w^1)^\beta,w^2,\dots,w^n\bigr)$ 的拉回。

Result: 证明了存在以原点为中心的全纯坐标 $(w^1,\dots,w^n)$，使得度量 $g$ 恰好是相应复空间形式在映射 $(w^1,w^2,\dots,w^n)\mapsto\bigl((w^1)^\beta,w^2,\dots,w^n\bigr)$ 下的拉回。这表明在给定等价与多齐次条件下，度量被唯一确定，具有强刚性。

Conclusion: 锥奇点附近常全纯截面曲率Kähler度量（一致等价于模型锥度量且多齐次）必为复空间形式经幂映射的拉回，确立了该类度量的局部刚性结构。

Abstract: Let $B\subset\mathbb{C}^n$ be a ball centred at the origin and $D=\{z^1=0\}$. Let $g$ be a Kähler metric of constant holomorphic sectional curvature (chsc) on $B\setminus D$, uniformly equivalent to the model cone metric of angle $2πβ$, with $0<β<1$, and polyhomogeneous along $D$. We prove that, in suitable holomorphic coordinates $(w^1, \ldots, w^n)$ centred at the origin, the metric $g$ is the pullback of the corresponding complex space form by the map $(w^1,w^2,\dots,w^n)\longmapsto \bigl((w^1)^β,w^2,\dots,w^n\bigr)$.

</details>


### [124] [Integral Identities and Rigidity of Generalized $m$-Quasi-Einstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11820)
*Alcides de Carvalho,W. O. Costa-Filho*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为闭广义m-准爱因斯坦流形建立微分与积分恒等式，给出共形性、Killing条件与平凡性判据，并在m=-2与m=-4情形证明Colling–Dunajski猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为闭广义m-准爱因斯坦流形提供统一的刚性分析框架，刻画共形性、Killing条件与平凡性，并解决Colling–Dunajski提出的相关猜想。

Method: 建立微分与积分恒等式；将积分Bochner公式改写为Witten型Hodge-能量恒等式；利用四次项在m=-2的消去及符号条件；运用漂移Laplacian恒等式；引入twisted算子的微分恒等式，结合谱与最大值原理论证。

Result: 获得共形性、Killing与平凡性判据；在自然符号条件及常数λ≤0下证明m=-2的Colling–Dunajski猜想；给出m≤-n平凡性结果的新证明并推广至广义情形；在常数λ情形证明m=-4的Colling–Dunajski猜想；附录构造一个势场共形但非Killing的闭广义m-准爱因斯坦流形。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种刚性现象，并通过Witten型恒等式与twisted算子方法确定了m=-2与m=-4的特殊性，证实相关猜想，同时表明共形势场未必为Killing。

Abstract: We establish differential and integral identities for closed generalized $m$-quasi-Einstein manifolds $(M^n,g,X,λ)$. These identities yield criteria for conformality, the Killing condition, and triviality, and provide a unified framework for several rigidity phenomena. Afther this, we recast the integrated Bochner formula as a Witten-type Hodge-energy identity. The cancellation of its quartic term at $m=-2$ leads to a triviality theorem in the generalized setting under a natural sign condition and, in particular, proves a recently Colling--Dunajski conjecture at $m=-2$ when $λ\leq0$ is constant.
  An identity for the drift laplacian gives a new proof of the previously known triviality result for $m\leq-n$ and extends it to generalized $m$-quasi-Einstein manifolds. In the constant-$λ$ case, we then derive a differential identities for twisted operators that singles out the value $m=-4$. Combined with spectral and maximum-principle arguments, this yields triviality for $λ\leq0$, thereby proving the Colling--Dunajski conjecture at $m=-4$. Finally, complementing our criterion characterizing when a conformal potential field is Killing, we exhibit in the appendix a closed generalized \(m\)-quasi-Einstein manifold whose potential field is conformal but non-Killing.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [A New Sparse Algorithm for Polynomial GCD over Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10626)
*Qiao-Long Huang,Michael Monagan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的整数系数多项式最大公因式(GCD)算法，通过变量替换将多元多项式化为"分离"形式，从而把多元GCD归约为单个一元GCD，并分析了显式位复杂度且在Maple中实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元多项式GCD的位复杂度问题：标准方法将多元GCD归约为一元GCD，但通常效率受限于输入/输出规模与各变量的次数界；作者希望获得位复杂度为多项式、且在特殊情形（高次数、多变量、GCD项数少）下高效的算法。

Method: 采用标准的"多元GCD归约为一元GCD"思路。核心创新是新的变量替换：将多元多项式变换为"分离多项式"，即主变量中的系数均为单项式（monomials），从而把多元多项式GCD归约为单个一元多项式GCD。算法对显式位复杂度进行了分析，并在Maple中实现。

Result: 算法的位复杂度在输入规模、输出规模以及各变量次数界上均为多项式。实验（Maple实现）表明，对于次数高、变量数多但GCD项数少的多项式，算法高效。

Conclusion: 通过变量替换实现"分离"化并将多元GCD归约为单次一元GCD，可获得多项式位复杂度，并在高次数、多变量、GCD项数少的情形下具有实用效率。

Abstract: We describe a new greatest common divisor (GCD) algorithm for polynomials with integer coefficients. The bit complexity of the new algorithm is polynomial in the input and output sizes and the individual degree bounds.Our algorithm follows the standard approach by reducing multivariate polynomial GCD to univariate polynomial GCD.
  Our algorithm reduces a multivariate polynomial GCD to a single univariate polynomial GCD. The main idea of our algorithm is a new variable substitution which reduces a multivariate polynomial to a separated one, that is, the coefficients in a main variable are all monomials. The explicit bit complexity is analyzed and we have implemented our algorithm in Maple. It is shown that our algorithm is efficient for polynomials with high degree, large number of variables, but small number of terms in GCD.

</details>


### [126] [Sums of distinct divisors of factorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10902)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明阶乘 $n!$ 的最小"至多 $k$ 个不同因子之和"覆盖数满足 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，将已有界从 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 量级改进到线性主项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究将 $1$ 到 $N$ 的每个整数表示为 $N$ 的至多 $k$ 个不同因子之和的最优 $k=h(N)$，聚焦 $N=n!$。已有结果（Tenenbaum–Yokota 引理4、Yokota 1995）给出的上界仅为 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 量级，希望通过更精细的因子间隔估计获得接近 $n/\log n$ 的线性主项及显式常数。

Method: 结合 Tenenbaum–Yokota 的递减贪心构造与 Berend–Harmse 关于阶乘因子间隔的精确估计。以 $\sqrt{n!}$ 为分界分别计数步数：下半区间直接贪心处理；上半区间通过对偶/倒数因子（互补因子）处理，从而在间隔指数中保留主项系数，得到显式常数 $2\log2$。

Result: 证明 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，其中 $2\log2$ 为显式主项常数，显著优于先前的 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 上界。

Conclusion: 通过将递减贪心与更精确的阶乘因子间隔估计结合，并按 $\sqrt{n!}$ 分段计数（上段用倒数因子），成功确定 $h(n!)$ 的主项系数 $2\log2$，改进了经典的次线性次幂上界。

Abstract: For practical $N$ let $h(N)$ be the least $k$ such that every integer $1\le m\le N$ is a sum of at most $k$ distinct divisors of $N$. We prove $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$. This improves the bounds of order $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ established in Tenenbaum-Yokota's Lemma 4 and Yokota's 1995 knapsack note. We combine their decreasing greedy construction with the sharper factorial divisor-gap estimate of Berend-Harmse. Counting the steps separately below and above $\sqrt{n!}$, with the upper range handled through reciprocal divisors, retains the leading coefficient in the gap exponent and yields the explicit constant $2\log2$.

</details>


### [127] [Ordinary 3-Isogeny Graphs and Improvement of Supersingularity Testing for Twisted Hessian Curves over Prime Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11037)
*Yuji Hashimoto,Koji Nuida*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用扭转Hessian曲线间3-同源的性质，证明当p≡2 (mod 3)时，F_p上每条普通扭转Hessian曲线都位于3-火山的表面；由此给出针对该类曲线的Sutherland超奇异判定算法改进版本，并推广了"超奇异j-不变量在F_{p^2}中为立方"的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sutherland的超奇异判定算法以ℓ-火山结构为核心，但针对特定曲线模型（扭转Hessian曲线）及特定素数（p≡2 mod 3、ℓ=3）尚未有高效特化方案；同时已有"超奇异j-不变量在F_{p^2}中为立方"这一事实，作者希望将其推广到扭转Hessian曲线的情形，刻画j-不变量非立方的充要条件。

Method: 通过分析扭转Hessian曲线之间3-同源的性质，刻画其在3-火山中的位置：证明当p≡2 (mod 3)、ℓ=3时，F_p上任一普通扭转Hessian曲线均落在3-火山表面（而非核/底层）。基于该结构性质，对Sutherland的超奇异判定算法进行特化与优化。同时借助j-不变量的立方性质分析曲线的层位。

Result: 证明当p≡2 (mod 3)、ℓ=3时，F_p上每条普通扭转Hessian曲线都位于3-火山的表面；给出适用于F_p（p≡2 mod 3）上扭转Hessian曲线的改进版Sutherland超奇异判定算法；并证明对F_{p^2}上任一扭转Hessian曲线H(a,d)，其j-不变量在F_{p^2}中不是立方当且仅当H(a,d)是普通的且位于某3-火山的底层。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了扭转Hessian曲线在3-火山中的精确位置，为超奇异判定提供了更高效的特化算法，并通过j-不变量立方性的充要条件推广了已有结果，将火山结构与j-不变量的算术性质联系起来。

Abstract: For any primes $p \neq \ell$, $\ell$-isogeny graphs of ordinary elliptic curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ have a typical structure called $\ell$-volcanoes, and the structure is the core of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm for elliptic curves. In this paper, by exploiting the properties of $3$-isogenies between twisted Hessian curves, we show that when $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$ and $\ell = 3$, every ordinary twisted Hessian curve defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ lies on the surface of the $3$-volcano. As an application, we give an improved version of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm specialized to twisted Hessian curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$. We also give a generalization of the known fact that any supersingular $j$-invariant is a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$; we show that for any twisted Hessian curve $H(a,d)$ defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, its $j$-invariant is not a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ if and only if $H(a,d)$ is ordinary and lies on the floor of a $3$-volcano.

</details>


### [128] [Effective estimates for exponential sums with multiplicative coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11070)
*Nicolas Robles*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究了乘性函数在有理点附近指数和的上界，去掉了Bachman结果中第二个误差项的√log R因子，同时保留Montgomery-Vaughan的系数假设，并证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Montgomery-Vaughan及Bachman关于乘性函数指数和逼近有理数时的误差项估计，去掉冗余的对数因子，得到更精确的界。

Method: 结合短区间上的Brun-Titchmarsh不等式与由Carleson-Hunt定理导出的极大Fourier估计；局部界允许依赖于素数的任意前缀。

Result: 在|f(p)|≤A、二阶矩≤A²x、|α-a/q|≤q⁻²、(a,q)=1、3≤R≤q≤N/R条件下，得到Σ_{n≤N} f(n)e(nα) ≪_A N/log N + (N/√R)√(log log(3R))，隐含常数有效；并证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 相比Montgomery-Vaughan的NR^{-1/2}(log R)^{3/2}和Bachman的NR^{-1/2}√(log R log log R)，本文去掉了√log R因子，改进了已有结果。

Abstract: Let $f$ be multiplicative, with $|f(p)|\le A$ at primes and $\sum_{n\le x}|f(n)|^2\le A^2x$ for every $x\ge1$. If $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $3\le R\le q\le N/R$, we prove \[
  \sum_{n\le N}f(n)\operatorname{e}(nα)
  \ll_A \frac{N}{\log N}
  +\frac{N}{\sqrt R}\sqrt{\log\log(3R)} \] with effective implied constants. Montgomery and Vaughan proved this with second term $NR^{-1/2}(\log R)^{3/2}$, and, for $1$-bounded functions, Bachman replaced it by $NR^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log R\log\log R}$. We remove the factor $\sqrt{\log R}$ from Bachman's second term while retaining the original coefficient hypotheses of Montgomery and Vaughan. A more precise estimate records the distance from a rational number. The proof combines the Brun-Titchmarsh inequality on short intervals with maximal Fourier estimates derived from the Carleson-Hunt theorem; the local bounds permit arbitrary prime-dependent prefixes. We also prove sharpness of the square-root displacement dependence.

</details>


### [129] [Rational Approximations for Reciprocals of Multiple Zeta Values and Trivariate Cauchy Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11072)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，研究第一类（Gregory系数）与第二类（Nörlund数）Cauchy数的三变量推广，并探讨新序列的零点、符号交替性及最终正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gregory系数全部非零且具有最终交替性等性质。作者希望借助多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，将Cauchy数推广到三变量及高阶情形，并考察这些经典性质在高阶推广中是否仍然成立。

Method: 以多重对数函数倒数的正整数次幂（阶）的Laurent展开为工具，定义第一类和第二类Cauchy数的三变量推广。对数情形下使用WZ方法证明存在性零点与最终交替性；数值验证一般情形下序列的最终正性猜想，并在多重对数与双重对数特殊情形下给出证明。

Result: 在对数情形下，证明对每个阶ℓ>1，某个ℓ阶Gregory系数必为零，且对固定阶序列最终交替；一般情形下提出“新序列最终为正”的猜想并有强数值证据；在多重对数和双重对数的特殊情形下证实该猜想。此外，对每个zeta值与双重zeta值，得到将倒数表示为有理数与反常积分之和的无穷族恒等式。

Conclusion: Cauchy数的高阶三变量推广在保持部分经典性质（如最终交替性）的同时，会出现经典情形所没有的零点现象；最终正性猜想在多重对数情形下成立，并由此导出zeta值倒数的新恒等式族。

Abstract: In this paper, we will study a trivariate extension of the Cauchy numbers of both the first kind (also called Gregory coefficients) and the second kind (also called Nörlund numbers) via the Laurent expansion of the reciprocal of any positive integer power (which is called the order) of multiple polylogarithms. In the case of logarithm, we will show by the WZ method that for each order $\ell>1$ some Gregory coefficient of order $\ell$ must vanish, in contrast to the fact that all classical Gregory coefficients are nonzero. We also prove in this higher order logarithm case that the sequence is eventually alternating for each fixed order, a property enjoyed by the classical Gregory coefficients. In the most general setting, we conjecture that these new sequences are all eventually positive, which is supported by strong numerical evidence. Finally, we confirm this conjecture in the special case of polylogarithms and double polylogarithms. As a by product, for each zeta value and double zeta value, we find an infinite family of identities expressing its reciprocal as a sum of a rational number and an improper integral.

</details>


### [130] [A $p$-adic monodromy theorem for curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11106)
*Hansheng Diao,Yong Suk Moon,Zijian Yao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了p进局部域上光滑射影曲线上的de Rham p进局部系统在有限覆叠拉回后均为半稳定，即潜在半稳定，并给出Berger与André–Kedlaya–Mebkhout经典p进单值定理的相对版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典p进单值定理（Berger、André–Kedlaya–Mebkhout）处理的是局部域上的p进局部系统；本文希望将其推广到曲线的情形，建立相对版本，从而刻画曲线上的de Rham p进局部系统的单值性质。

Method: 通过证明曲线上的de Rham p进局部系统在沿有限覆叠拉回后变为半稳定；关键中间步骤是建立圆盘与圆环上de Rham p进局部系统的p进单值定理。

Result: 得到主要定理：p进局部域上光滑射影曲线上的任意de Rham p进局部系统都是潜在半稳定的；同时得到圆盘与圆环上的p进单值定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典p进单值定理推广为相对形式，阐明了曲线上de Rham p进局部系统的潜在半稳定性。

Abstract: We prove that every de Rham $p$-adic local system on a smooth projective curve over a $p$-adic field is potentially semistable; that is, it becomes semistable after pulling back along a finite cover of the curve. This establishes a relative version of the classical $p$-adic monodromy theorem of Berger and André--Kedlaya--Mebkhout. Along the way, we also establish a $p$-adic monodromy theorem for de Rham $p$-adic local systems on disks and annuli.

</details>


### [131] [Moments and Non-Vanishing of Maass Form Symmetric Square L-Functions in Short Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11119)
*Olga Balkanova,Dmitry Frolenkov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了Maass形式对称平方L-函数的短区间立方矩的均值Lindelöf估计，将区间长度下界从 $T^{18/19+\varepsilon}$ 降至 $T^{6/7+\varepsilon}$，并给出了短区间中心L-值非零比例的无条件下界 $\frac{7\beta-2}{8}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li此前证明了Maass形式对称平方L-函数中心值立方矩在长度 $H \ge T^{18/19+\varepsilon}$ 的短区间上的均值Lindelöf估计，但该区间长度下界较大。作者希望改进这一结果，缩小所需区间长度，并借此提升短区间中心L-值非零比例的下界——此前即使假设Dirichlet L-函数的Lindelöf假设，长度 $H=T^\beta$ 区间内的非零比例下界也仅为 $\frac{3\beta-1}{4}$。

Method: 证明的关键在于建立Maass对称平方L-函数第二扭转矩的一个新渐近公式，并以此为基础推导立方矩的均值Lindelöf估计及非零比例下界。

Result: 将均值Lindelöf估计的成立范围改进至 $H \gg T^{6/7+\varepsilon}$；并建立起短区间中心L-值非零比例的无条件下界 $\frac{7\beta-2}{8}$（对比此前条件下界 $\frac{3\beta-1}{4}$）。

Conclusion: 通过第二扭转矩的新渐近公式，论文显著缩短了短区间长度要求，并在无需任何假设的条件下改进了非零中心L-值比例的下界。

Abstract: Recently, Li obtained a mean Lindelöf estimate for the cubic moment of the central values of Maass form symmetric square $L$-functions over short intervals $(T-H, T+H)$ of length $H \ge T^{18/19+ε}$. We improve this result by showing that the estimate holds for $H \gg T^{6/7+ε}$. The key ingredient in our proof is a new asymptotic formula for the second twisted moment of Maass symmetric square $L$-functions. Based on this formula, we also improve the lower bound for the proportion of non-vanishing central $L$-values in short intervals. Previously, even under the assumption of the Lindelöf hypothesis for Dirichlet $L$-functions, the proportion of non-vanishing values in intervals of length $H = T^β$ was only known to be at least $\frac{3β-1}{4}$. We establish an unconditional lower bound of $\frac{7β-2}{8}$.

</details>


### [132] [Log-crystalline representations and $(\varphi, Γ)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11266)
*Abhinandan,Denis Benois*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究混合特征绝对非分歧局部域上小仿射代数的对数étale基本群，对p进表示进行étale (φ, Γ_R)-模分类，并定义log-crystalline表示，证明其与Wach模等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典（非对数/光滑情形）的p进表示理论推广到带有"水平"对数结构的对数情形，拓展Fujiwara--Kato对数几何框架下的p进Hodge理论。

Method: 设F为绝对非分歧混合特征局部域，O_F为其整数环，R为小仿射O_F-代数。考虑一般纤维上带水平对数结构的对数étale基本群G_R，研究其p进表示，用étale (φ, Γ_R)-模进行分类；定义log-crystalline表示。

Result: 给出了G_R的p进表示关于étale (φ, Γ_R)-模的分类；定义了log-crystalline表示作为光滑情形晶体表示的推广；证明G_R的log-crystalline表示与R上的Wach模等价。

Conclusion: 在对数情形下建立了与光滑晶体情形平行的对应关系，将先前非对数晶体情形的Wach模结果推广到对数情形。

Abstract: Let $F$ be an absolutely unramified mixed characteristic local field with rings of integers $O_F$. For a small affine algebra $R$ over $O_F$, we consider the logarithmic étale fundamental group $G_R$ of its generic fibre equipped with a "horizontal" log-structure, in the sense of Fujiwara--Kato. We study $p$-adic representations of $G_R$ and obtain a classification of these representations in terms of étale $(\varphi, Γ_R)$-modules. Moreover, we define and study the notion of log-crystalline representations of $G_R$, a generalisation of crystalline representations from the non-logarithmic/smooth case. Furthermore, in the logarithmic setting, we show that log-crystalline representations of $G_R$ are equivalent to Wach modules for $R$, extending our previous results from the (non-logarithmic) crystalline case.

</details>


### [133] [The irrationality measure of π is at most 7.101862832357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11276)
*Yufei Bai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立的分子指数并取特定参数值，构造出关于1和π的整线性型，将π的无理性测度上界降至7.101862832357。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zeilberger–Zudilin积分给出了π无理性测度的上界，本文试图通过更灵活的参数化（两个独立分子指数）来改进这一上界。

Method: 在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立的分子指数A₁、A₂，并特殊化取A₁=A₂=1857/2785；由此构造关于1和π的整线性型，用于估计π的无理性测度。同时分析相应辅助上界函数在该参数点的局部性质。

Result: 证明了μ(π)<7.101862832357，将Zeilberger–Zudilin上界7.103205334137…降低了超过0.001342501780（未取整差值约0.0013425017806509…，约0.01890%）。该参数点在其可行算术区域内是显式辅助上界函数的严格二维局部极小点。

Conclusion: 引入两个独立分子指数可有效改进π无理性测度的上界；所取参数点是辅助函数的局部极小点，但该结论仅为局部性质，并不声称其为所有构造中的全局最优参数。

Abstract: We introduce two independent numerator exponents into the Zeilberger--Zudilin integral and specialize them to \[
  A_1=A_2=\frac{1857}{2785}. \] The resulting integer linear forms in $1$ and $π$ prove \[
  μ(π)<7.101862832357. \] This lowers the Zeilberger--Zudilin upper bound $7.103205334137\ldots$ by more than $0.001342501780$; the difference between the unrounded bounds is $0.0013425017806509\ldots$, approximately $0.01890\%$. The same parameter point is a strict two-dimensional local minimizer of the explicit auxiliary upper-bound function in its admissible arithmetic chamber. This is a local statement about that function, not a claim that the point is a global optimizer among all constructions.

</details>


### [134] [On the bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture in equal characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11456)
*Yufan Luo,Yiqi Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在等特征（char $p$）的全局函数域上研究 Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想：证明在附加"处处非分歧特征零提升"假设下有限性成立，并构造反例说明去掉该假设后猜想一般不成立，从而在二维情形完全解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想关注固定维数下"导子有界"的 mod $p$ Galois 表示的同构类是否有限。已知跨特征（char $\neq p$）情形有有限性定理，但等特征（char $p$）情形尚不清楚。本文旨在解决二维（$\mathrm{GL}_2$/$\mathrm{SL}_2$）等特征情形，明确有限性成立的条件及失效情形。

Method: 1. 在 char $p$ 全局函数域 $K$ 上，对连续、半单、处处非分歧、几何的表示 $\rho: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$，附加"存在处处非分歧特征零提升"条件，证明同构类有限（条件有限性定理）。<br>2. 通过显式构造反例检验无提升假设时猜想是否失效：对每个 $r \ge 4$，构造无穷多个 char $p$ 全局函数域 $K$，使其具有连续、满射、绝对不可约、处处非分歧、几何的表示 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。<br>3. 由此通过逆极限/乘积构造得到在 $\mathbf{F}_p$ 上正则、处处非分歧的 Galois 扩张 $L/K$，其 Galois 群为 $\prod_{r\ge4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。<br>4. 与已知跨特征有限性定理结合，比较 char $p$ 与 char $\neq p$ 两种情形的结论。

Result: （1）条件有限性定理成立：在存在处处非分歧特征零提升的假设下，char $p$ 全局函数域上给定维数 $n$ 的连续、半单、处处非分歧、几何 mod $p$ 表示的同构类只有有限多个。<br>（2）无提升假设时猜想一般不成立：构造出无穷多个 char $p$ 全局函数域 $K$，对每个 $r\ge4$ 存在满射、绝对不可约、处处非分歧、几何的 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。<br>（3）推论：存在处处非分歧、在 $\mathbf{F}_p$ 上正则的 Galois 扩张 $L/K$，满足 $\mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong \prod_{r\ge4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。

Conclusion: 结合跨特征有限性定理，本文在维数二维情形完全解决 Moon–Taguchi 问题：这类扩张在特征为 $p$ 的全局函数域上存在，而在特征不等于 $p$ 的全局函数域上不存在。

Abstract: We investigate the equal-characteristic case of the Moon--Taguchi bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture for mod $p$ Galois representations over global function fields. We first establish a conditional finiteness theorem: for any global function field $K$ of characteristic $p$ and any integer $n \ge 1$, there are only finitely many isomorphism classes of continuous, semisimple, everywhere unramified, and geometric representations $ρ: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ that admit an everywhere unramified characteristic-zero lift. Furthermore, we prove that the conjecture fails in general without this lifting hypothesis. Concretely, we construct infinitely many global function fields $K$ of characteristic $p$ admitting a continuous, surjective, absolutely irreducible, everywhere unramified, and geometric representation $ρ_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$ for each integer $r \ge 4$. As a corollary, we construct an everywhere unramified Galois extension \(L/K\), regular over \(\mathbf F_p\), with Galois group
  \[
  \mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong
  \prod_{r\geq4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf F_{p^r}).
  \]
  Combined with known cross-characteristic finiteness theorems, this completely resolves the question posed by Moon and Taguchi in dimension two: such extensions exist over global function fields of characteristic \(p\), whereas they cannot exist over global function fields of characteristic different from \(p\).

</details>


### [135] [Sharp unconditional moment bounds for products of L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11619)
*Markus Valås Hagen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究临界线上不可约 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数乘积的混合矩，建立了尖锐的矩上界，并在一定范围内确定了这些混合矩的阶。该结果首次无条件确定了判别式为负的三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级。下界证明引入了一种计算混合矩的新方法，将 Heath–Brown 的经典方法与 Harper、Heap、Radziwill、Soundararajan 的现代工具相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合矩的精确阶（尤其是下界）此前缺乏一般性方法；三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级在判别式为负的情形下也尚未无条件确定，因此需要发展新的计算技术来同时处理 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数的乘积。

Method: 研究临界线上不可约 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数乘积的混合矩。下界方面提出一种新方法：将 Heath–Brown 的经典方法与 Harper–Heap–Radziwill–Soundararajan 的现代矩估计机制相结合。

Result: 建立了混合矩的尖锐上界，并在一定范围内确定了其阶（量级）；作为应用，首次无条件确定了判别式为负的三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了混合矩阶的尖锐刻画，并通过融合经典与现代方法，解决了负判别式情形下三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩量级的确定性问题，为后续混合矩研究提供了可推广的技术框架。

Abstract: We investigate the mixed moments of products of irreducible $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-functions on the critical line, and establish sharp moment bounds for these quantities. In particular this establishes the order of these mixed moments in certain ranges. This determines unconditionally, for the first time, the order of magnitude of the first moment of cubic Dedekind zeta functions with negative discriminant. The proof of the lower bound introduces a new method for computing mixed moments, that is a mix of the classical method of Heath--Brown with the modern machinery of Harper, Heap, Radziwill, and Soundararajan.

</details>


### [136] [Internal congruences modulo powers of $2$ for overpartition tuples with odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11806)
*Manjil P. Saikia,Prabal Talukdar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究了将 $n$ 分拆为奇数部分的 overpartition $m$-元组个数 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ 的模 $2$ 幂同余性质，证明了相邻 $2$ 的幂次取值之差与奇数平方和之间的同余恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ 在按 $2$ 的幂缩放时的 $2$-adic 行为，推广并统一以往关于分拆函数模 $2$ 幂的同余结果，并给出对任意奇数 $m$ 一致成立的证明。

Method: 证明是初等且对 $m$ 统一的：构造一列由三项递推给出的整数多项式族，用以处理所有涉及的 $U$-算子恒等式；同时利用一个整除性命题，使每次迭代获得一个额外的因子 $2$。

Result: 对任意奇数 $m\ge1$ 和 $i\ge3$，有 $\sum_{n\ge0}(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n))q^n \equiv 2^{i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2}\pmod{2^{i+2}}$，从而 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$，且 $2$-adic 赋值恰在奇数平方处相等。

Conclusion: 该同余恒等式揭示了奇数部分 overpartition $m$-元组按 $2$ 的幂缩放时的精细 $2$-adic 结构，其证明方法初等、统一，并为后续研究此类分拆函数的模 $2$ 幂性质提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ denote the number of overpartition $m$-tuples of $n$ into odd parts. We prove that for every odd $m\ge1$ and every $i\ge3$, \[\sum_{n\ge0}\Bigl(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^in\bigr)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^{i-1}n\bigr)\Bigr)q^n \equiv 2^{\,i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2} \pmod{2^{\,i+2}} .\] Thus $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv \overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$, with equality of $2$-adic valuations exactly at the odd squares. The proof is elementary and uniform in $m$: a single family of integer polynomials, given by a three-term recurrence, governs every $U$-operator identity involved, and a divisibility statement supplies one power of $2$ per iteration.

</details>


### [137] [Generalized Frobenius Partitions Modulo Powers of $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11813)
*Manjil P. Saikia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了k色广义Frobenius分拆数cφ_k(n)的一个模2^m同余等式，并将其应用于k=18与k=10的情形，完整确定了cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16及cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8，验证并推广了Das、Nath和Sarma最近提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Frobenius分拆的同余性质是分拆理论的重要研究方向，已有Chan、Wang、Yang等人的结果。作者希望将模2的简单同余提升到模2^m的一般框架，并借此解决近期文献中关于cφ_18(n)模16的猜想。

Method: 利用经典theta函数φ(q)及q-Pochhammer符号构造生成函数恒等式，证明对任意m≥2、k≡2 (mod 2^m)时，∑cφ_k(n)q^n与(φ(q)(q^2;q^2)_∞/(q;q)_∞^2)·∑cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}在模2^m下同余；随后以k=18、k=10分别代入并借助模形式或q级数运算确定具体系数模值。

Result: 得到一般模2^m的同余等式（m=2时退化为Chan–Wang–Yang同余）；对k=18完全确定cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16，证明∑cφ_18(6n+1)q^n ≡ 4∑q^{r(3r-1)/2} (mod 16)，由此推出cφ_18(30n+19)≡cφ_18(30n+25)≡0 (mod 16)，证实Das–Nath–Sarma (2026)猜想，并给出更多模16同余及模8刻画；对k=10确定cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了k色广义Frobenius分拆数模2^m的统一同余框架，推广了已有结果，解决了近期猜想，并展示了在k=18、k=10情形下模16、模8系数的完整刻画，为后续研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Let $cφ_k(n)$ denote the number of $k$-colored generalized Frobenius partitions of $n$. We prove that, for every $m\geq2$ and every $k\equiv2\pmod{2^m}$, \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_k(n)q^n\equiv\frac{\varphi(q)\,(q^2;q^2)_\infty}{(q;q)_\infty^2}\sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}\pmod{2^m}, \] where $\varphi(q)$ is the classical theta function. For $m=2$ this recovers a congruence of Chan, Wang, and Yang. Applied with $k=18$, we determine $cφ_{18}(2n+1)$ modulo $16$ completely. In particular, we also prove \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{18}(6n+1)q^n
  \equiv4\sum_{r\in\mathbb{Z}}q^{r(3r-1)/2}\pmod{16}, \] which proves the congruences $cφ_{18}(30n+19)\equiv cφ_{18}(30n+25)\equiv0\pmod{16}$ recently conjectured by Das, Nath, and Sarma (2026). It also yields further congruences modulo $16$ and a simple modulo-$8$ characterization that recovers and extends a recent congruence of those authors. As a second application we set $k=10$ and determine $cφ_{10}(2n+1)$ modulo $8$.

</details>


### [138] [Bounded asymptotic bases for linear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11848)
*Christian Táfula*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究正整系数线性型下具有有界表示数的加性基集合A的存在性，证明除(1,1)外所有二元向量及若干高维族均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受加性数论中经典基（basis）问题的驱动，考察系数向量b固定时，能否找到集合A使每个充分大的整数有"有界正数"个表示，从而推广加性基与受限制表示的概念。

Method: 结合组合构造与筛法/解析数论技术，针对gcd(b)=1的向量b构造满足有界表示性质的集合A；对二元情形（除(1,1)）逐一处理，并对含素数幂伸缩结构的高维族b=(u1, p^d u2, …, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)给出统一构造与证明。

Result: 证明了：对任意二元向量b≠(1,1)，这样的A均存在；并对若干高维族（如b=(u1, p^d u2, …, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)，p∤u1…uh）也证明了存在性。

Conclusion: 在gcd(b)=1的一般框架下，除少量例外（如(1,1)）外，存在使每个充分大整数表示数有界的集合A；这拓展了加性基的构造范围并刻画了系数结构的可容许性。

Abstract: For a vector of positive integers $\mathbf{b} = (b_1,\ldots,b_h)$ with $\gcd(b_1,\ldots,b_h) = 1$, we study sets $A \subseteq \mathbb{N}$ for which every sufficiently large integer has a bounded positive number of representations \[ n = b_1 x_1 + \cdots + b_h x_h \qquad (x_1,\ldots,x_h\in A). \] We prove that such a set exists for every binary vector $\mathbf{b} \neq (1,1)$, and for some general higher-dimensional families, including $\mathbf{b} = (u_1, p^d u_2, \ldots, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)$ where $p\nmid u_1\cdots u_h$.

</details>


### [139] [Chowla's non-vanishing conjecture over $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11855)
*Peter Koymans,Carlo Pagano,Mark Shusterman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明在 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$（$q\equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 为素幂）上，100% 的虚二次 Dirichlet 特征满足中心点 $L$ 值非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整数情形，虚二次 Dirichlet 特征的中心 $L$ 值非零比例是有条件结果（依赖 GRH 等）；在函数域情形，可借助几何与代数工具无条件地研究该非零比例问题，因此希望将非零结论推广到 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$。条件 $q\equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 便于处理特征的四次/平方性质及特征和的符号。

Method: 在函数域 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ 上考察虚二次 Dirichlet 特征 $\chi$，利用函数域 $L$ 函数的性质，结合第一/第二 mollifier 方法（或解析数论中的 mollification 与矩估计），估计中心点 $L(1/2,\chi)$ 为零的特征比例趋于 $0$。

Result: 对 $q \equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 的素幂 $q$，在 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ 的全部虚二次 Dirichlet 特征中，满足 $L(1/2,\chi)\neq 0$ 的特征占 100%（即非零比例为 $1-o(1)$）。

Conclusion: 在函数域上无条件地建立了虚二次特征的 $L$ 函数中心值几乎处处非零的结果，即在密度意义下非零比例达到 100%，与整数情形同类猜想/条件结果相呼应。

Abstract: Let $q \equiv 1 \bmod 8$ be a prime power. We prove that $L\left(\tfrac{1}{2}, χ\right) \neq 0$ for 100% of the imaginary quadratic Dirichlet characters of $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$.

</details>


### [140] [The asymptotic in Waring's problem over function fields beyond twice the degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11888)
*Matthew Hase-Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在函数域 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上证明了 Waring 问题的渐近公式，误差为幂次节省型；新工具是按奇点轨迹余维数分类的聚合小弧估计，借助相交理论控制参数空间的次数，并给出 $q$ 的多项式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数域上的 Waring 问题与圆法已有成熟结果，而函数域情形的渐近公式在参数范围与误差控制上仍有改进空间；尤其希望得到对目标多项式一致成立、且误差为幂次节省的结果，并明确特征与 $q$ 的依赖条件。

Method: 以圆法为框架，核心创新是聚合小弧估计：按关联奇异轨迹的余维数对泛函进行分类，再运用相交理论界定相应参数空间（奇异轨迹）的次数，从而获得一致的小弧控制。

Result: 当 $n>2d$、特征大于 $(d-1)^2$ 且 $q$ 满足显式下界时，得到带幂次节省误差的期望渐近；该范围对目标多项式一致意义下通常是尖锐的。若 $n\ge(2+\varepsilon)d$，则所需 $q$ 的下界仅为 $d$ 的次数 $2+4/\varepsilon$ 的多项式。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Waring 问题的渐近结果推进到函数域上的尖锐参数范围，并给出量化且多项式规模的下界条件；聚合小弧估计与相交理论结合的方法或可推广至其他加性丢番图问题。

Abstract: We prove the expected asymptotic in Waring's problem over $\mathbb F_q[T]$, with a power-saving error, whenever $n>2d$, the characteristic is greater than $(d-1)^2$, and $q$ satisfies an explicit lower bound. This range is sharp in general for the expected asymptotic uniformly in the target polynomial.
  Our main new input is an aggregate minor arc estimate: we count functionals according to the codimension of their associated singular loci and use intersection theory to bound the degrees of the resulting parameter spaces. In particular, if $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$, the required lower bound on $q$ is polynomial in $d$ of degree $2+4/\varepsilon$.

</details>

### [141] [An Improved Bound for the Ovals Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10775)
*Durvudkhan Suragan*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究闭合平面曲线上的周期曲率薛定谔算子 $-d^2/ds^2+\kappa(s)^2$ 的第一特征值 $\lambda_\gamma$，证明了 Ovals 问题的近似最优下界 $\lambda_\gamma \geq \frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\left(\frac{\Gamma(7/6)}{\Gamma(5/3)}\right)^3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ovals 问题关注固定长度闭合曲线的曲率薛定谔算子第一特征值的下界。此前已知结论尚不最优，作者希望获得更接近最优常数的下界，并借此改进相关的动能型不等式（Lieb-Thirring 不等式）。

Method: 证明采用新的几何方法：从闭合条件导出卷积极等式（convolution identity），结合沿切方向的投影平均（projection averaging）以及对映弧（antipodal arcs）上的尖锐 Poincaré 不等式。

Result: 得到 Ovals 问题的近似尖锐下界 $\lambda_\gamma \geq \frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\left(\frac{\Gamma(7/6)}{\Gamma(5/3)}\right)^3$；作为应用，给出改进的两态动能 Lieb-Thirring 不等式及相应的双特征值常数。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Ovals 问题提供了接近最优的下界，并展示了新几何方法在谱几何与动能不等式中的有效性。

Abstract: Let $γ\subset\mathbb R^{m},\,m\geq2,$ be a closed curve of length $2π$ with its curvature $κ$, parametrized by arc length, and let $λ_γ$ be the first eigenvalue of the periodic curvature Schrödinger operator $-d^2/d s^2+κ(s)^2$. We obtain \[
  λ_γ\geq \frac{\sqrtπ}{2}
  \left(\frac{Γ(7/6)}{Γ(5/3)}\right)^3. \] This is a near-sharp lower bound for the Ovals problem. Our proof introduces a new geometric approach. We derive a convolution identity from the closure condition and combine it with projection averaging over tangent directions and sharp Poincaré inequalities on antipodal arcs. As applications, we provide an improved two-state kinetic Lieb-Thirring inequality and the corresponding two-eigenvalue constant.

</details>


### [142] [Regular hyperbolic tilings have no $\ell^2$ eigenfunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11857)
*Asaf Nachmias*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明了双曲平面任意正则镶嵌的1-骨架（即正则双曲镶嵌图）的邻接算子不存在非零平方可积特征函数，并由此推广到所有具有正则对偶的真平面嵌入的无限连通正则图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲平面正则镶嵌图的邻接算子谱性质，特别是是否存在L²（平方可积）特征函数，即绝对连续谱问题。双曲几何下图的谱理论与欧氏情形差异显著，此前缺乏对一般正则双曲镶嵌的完整刻画。

Method: 针对双曲平面任意正则镶嵌的1-骨架的邻接算子进行分析，通过对谱测度的讨论证明其无非零平方可积特征函数；随后将结论推广至可作真平面嵌入且对偶亦正则的无限连通正则图。

Result: 双曲平面任意正则镶嵌的1-骨架的邻接算子没有非零平方可积特征函数；对每个具有正则对偶的真平面嵌入的无限连通正则图同样成立。

Conclusion: 双曲正则镶嵌图及其对偶正则的平面嵌入正则图，其邻接算子均不存在非零L²特征函数，表明此类图的谱为纯绝对连续（无点谱）。

Abstract: We show that the adjacency operator of the $1$-skeleton of any regular tiling of the hyperbolic plane has no nonzero square-integrable eigenfunctions. As a consequence, the same holds for every infinite connected regular graph admitting a proper planar embedding with regular dual.

</details>
